# 第 28 章　记忆是梦的开场白

*3.8 版本主视觉　·　图源：米游社*

> Memories are the Prelude to Dreams　·　记忆是梦的开场白

## 224　Another One, Same One　另一个，同一个

> 世界：Astral Express　|　区域：Parlor Car　|　角色：The Dahlia; Trailblazer; Welt; Dan Heng; March 7th; Sunday; Himeko; Terminus　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Stranger in a Strange Land'' completed

### Discuss your next trip with the others

> *You've written down a miracle, and the days of Trailblazing are just ahead. Go talk to everyone about the upcoming journey.*

*(When entering the Parlor Car)*

**(Trailblazer):** Come to think of it, I haven't chatted with Sunny in ages. Let's go see what he's up to.

> **开拓者：**说来，很久没和老日一起聊聊了，去看看他在干什么吧。

**The Dahlia:** Let's start with the "First Question." Now, slowly turn around...

*(Quick Read)*

A Letter from Robin

Dear Brother, How are you doing these days? I had intended to visit you at Dewlight Pavilion as soon as possible upon my return, but with the approaching Charmony Festival and your busy schedule, I refrained from troubling you. However, an urgent matter compels me to share something with you immediately. Since my return to Penacony, I have experienced a peculiar change in my voice. At first, I thought it was caused by exhaustion or illness, but after consulting with doctors, they assured me of my perfect health and dismissed my concerns. However, my voice worsened over time, and I even experienced periods of complete voice loss. In order to find answers, I conducted many private investigations (using my idle time out of rehearsals, of course). Eventually, I realized that the Harmony in Penacony is not pure. A discord lurking within has tainted my voice of Harmony, which I believe to be the root cause of my vocal issues. I immediately realized that such levels of interference can only occur if either a powerful external force is pulling the strings, or if a senior member of The Family is involved. Unfortunately, further investigation has led me to the latter conclusion. This is an extremely alarming discovery: A traitor has emerged within The Family in Penacony, and it is highly likely that this person is one of the four family heads. (I trust you implicitly, dear brother, because of our promise). With the Charmony Festival on the horizon, I fear this person intends to impede its progress, or even use the festival for some ulterior motive. At any rate, I suggest you monitor the other family heads while also prioritizing your own safety. You are the only true family member I have left. There is another matter that requires our attention. During my investigation, I learned about the Memory Zone Meme "Death," and my further inquiries led me to believe that the culprit who directed it to cause this series of incidents is likely the aforementioned traitor in The Family. I have collected more clues and am prepared to verify my hypothesis. Rest assured, you can just focus on the preparations for the Charmony Festival. Once I've thoroughly investigated "Death," I'll come and meet you immediately. It won't take too long. Given your heavy workload, please take care of yourself. Don't stay in the Dreamscape all the time. Spend some time in reality when you are free. I've brought some more specialties from other galaxies: Giantmoa pudding tarts from Morillons, wild strawberries from Akonyako (known for their exceptional size and sweetness, which I'm certain you'll enjoy), and almond meringue cream cracknels from Medicia. Don't forget to enjoy them! May Xipe be with us! Your sister, Robin

> **大丽花：**无论那是什么，我相信，总会起到作用。在他们回来之前，不妨去找找看。

### Expose the Dreammaster's buried crimes

> *Gopher Wood, the Dreammaster, is the key to The Family's absolute power within the sweet dream. The dark crimes he personally buried might just be the breakthrough we're looking for.*

**The Dahlia:** It's that Memory Zone Meme. Be careful.

> **大丽花：**是那只忆域迷因，当心些。

*(Open the door)*

**The Dahlia:** What's that!? Firefly, let's go and check it out.

> **大丽花：**那是？！流萤，我们过去看看。

> **流萤：**看附近的痕迹，是「死亡」下的手。可它们…竟然真的死去了？

**The Dahlia:** No, your conjecture is right. It's just that these birds weren't welcome in another dreamscape.

> **大丽花：**不，你的猜想没错。只是，这些鸟儿不受另一片梦境欢迎。

**Firefly:** You've seen them before?

> **流萤：**你见过它们？

**The Dahlia:** Yes, but that's what makes this so intriguing. The outcome of the battle was the complete opposite of what I expected.

> **大丽花：**见过，可这反倒耐人寻味，战果和我预想中全然相反。

> **大丽花：**对了，有件很重要的事，我似乎还从没问过——

**Firefly:** ...

> **流萤：**……

**Firefly:** T-Today? Not really.

> **流萤：**今…天吗？还是不必了吧。

> **大丽花：**我就喜欢你猝不及防的样子，很可爱。

**The Dahlia:** Then... take a few steps back. I have a few conjectures. There might be some risk... If anything happens, don't worry about me.

> **大丽花：**那…稍微走开几步吧，我有一些猜想。会有些风险，如果发生了什么，不必管我。

**Firefly:** What are you...

> **流萤：**你这是……

**The Dahlia:** Just as I thought.

> **大丽花：**果然如此。

**Firefly:** So, what happened?

> **流萤：**所以发生了什么？

**The Dahlia:** These ravens are the incarnation of the Dreammaster Gopher Wood, that omnipotent being in the sweet dream. In front of him, even "Death" has no strength to resist.

> **大丽花：**这些乌鸫，正是「梦主」歌斐木的化身，那位在美梦中无所不能的存在。在他面前，「死亡」不该拥有反抗之力。

**The Dahlia:** The power I just released should have been enough for him to notice and deliver a fatal blow, yet here we are, completely unharmed.

> **大丽花：**我刚才释放的力量，已经足以被他察觉，降下致死一击，但我们却安然无恙。

**Firefly:** ...You know, you're quite similar to Blade sometimes. I mean... when you're staring death in the face.

> **流萤：**…有时候，你和刃还挺像。我是说…在可能死去的时候。

**The Dahlia:** It's not relief I feel. I'm savoring... a once-in-a-lifetime thrill.

> **大丽花：**我可不是带着如释重负的心情，我是在享受…一生仅有一次的刺激。

**Firefly:** If only it was just once for me too.

> **流萤：**我如果也只是一次就好了。

**Firefly:** But was it really him? If I remember correctly, the Dreammaster's body was burned to ashes in a riot long ago, and he can now only sustain his life within the sweet dream.

> **流萤：**不过，真的是他？我记得，很久之前的一场暴乱中，梦主的身躯已被焚毁，如今只能在美梦中延续生命。

**The Dahlia:** Not only that, over the long passage of time, he has merged himself with every inch of Penacony's land.

> **大丽花：**不仅如此，在漫长的时光中，他已将自身与匹诺康尼的每一寸土地融汇。

**The Dahlia:** The title "Dreammaster" doesn't mean he merely controls the dreamscape. Rather, it means he has already turned the dreamscape itself into his second body.

> **大丽花：**「梦主」二字，不是说他掌控了梦境——而是已让梦境本身，化作自己的第二副躯壳。

**The Dahlia:** In extreme cases, he could probably burn all of Penacony to the ground... Though hopefully he wouldn't have the heart to do it.

> **大丽花：**极端情况下，他恐怕能将整个匹诺康尼付之一炬——但愿他不会舍得。

**Firefly:** We better act fast.

> **流萤：**看来动作越快越好。

**The Dahlia:** Easy now, we're safe for the time being. Maybe it's not that he doesn't want to act against us, but rather that he can't. At least not yet?

> **大丽花：**别急，眼下我们安然无恙。或许他并非不想，而是暂且不能为难我们呢？

**The Dahlia:** Lately, Gopher Wood has been claiming to be ill, never appearing in the Twelve Hours. Yet just recently, he buried his darkest crime deep within the dreamscape.

> **大丽花：**近来歌斐木自称抱恙，从未在十二时刻现身。偏偏就在最近，他又在梦境深处，掩埋了自己最为阴暗的罪行。

**Firefly:** So you're saying that burying this crime took a heavy toll on him, leaving a flaw in his supposed omniscience and omnipotence... That does sound plausible. Could it have something to do with the Watchmaker's Legacy?

> **流萤：**你是说，掩埋这桩罪行，让他付出了很大的代价，让全知全能出现了缺陷…倒是很有可能。会和「钟表匠的遗产」有关吗？

**The Dahlia:** I don't think so. After all, that secret was branded as "the darkest." And perhaps... the "Legacy" is not one alone. Only by seeing it for ourselves can we know.

> **大丽花：**我不那么认为，毕竟，那秘密被冠上了「最为阴暗」的形容。也或许…「遗产」并非仅有一份。走吧，去亲眼看看，我们就能知道了。

> *The "Dreammaster" and Penacony are practically one and the same. Even though a particular incident has given us an opening to dig deeper, we should still proceed with caution.*

*(Approach the marked location)*

**The Dahlia:** A mistake anyone could make... Setting up layer upon layer of security around a secret only ends up exposing its flaws.

> **大丽花：**任何人都会犯下的错误——总为秘密设下重重警戒，反倒因此露出破绽。

**The Dahlia:** Why don't we give it a shot, and build up some experience dealing with memes?

> **大丽花：**要来试试吗，多积攒些面对迷因的经验？

**Firefly:** There won't be any new experiences. I'll activate Fyrefly Type-IV and end this quickly.

> **流萤：**不会有什么新经验的，我会启动「火萤IV型」，速战速决。

**The Dahlia:** What a pity. I would've much preferred to see you in a pinch, so you'd have to rely on me all the more.

> **大丽花：**真可惜——我更乐意看到你陷入窘境，然后对我更加依赖。

* Wave 2: Dreamjolt Troupe's Winder Goon ×2, Dreamjolt Troupe's Bubble Hound ×2

*(After the battle)*

**The Dahlia:** When it comes to this, you're the expert. Yet I'd always imagined that calling upon that power would make you... profoundly melancholic.

> **大丽花：**这方面，你才是专家呢。不过，我一直以为，使用那份力量会让你…惆怅万分。

**Firefly:** ...I'm not now, and never will be.

> **流萤：**…现在没有，以后也不会。

*(Approach the painting)*

**Firefly:** Is this it? An entrance?

> **流萤：**就是这里吗，一个入口？

> **大丽花：**只是伪装成一个入口。那道裂痕并非通往别处，而是一把记忆之锁，钥匙，则是承载特定记忆的一枚光锥。

**The Dahlia:** Clearly, Gopher Wood had help from a Memokeeper, and since that Light Cone isn't in our hands, I fear we'll be returning empty-handed.

> **大丽花：**显然，歌斐木得到过忆者的帮助——那枚光锥不在我们手中，恐怕要无功而返了。

> **流萤：**完全没有其他办法？

**The Dahlia:** We could try our luck. Perhaps the Memokeeper helping Gopher Wood shares the same habits as me.

> **大丽花：**可以碰碰运气，说不定协助歌斐木的忆者，和我有类似的习惯。

**The Dahlia:** I'll take a look, but don't get your hopes up.

> **大丽花：**我来找找看吧，别抱太大希望。

### Try opening the lock of memories

> *It seems The Dahlia has a way to open the lock of memories left by Gopher Wood... Let's go and see what she's up to.*

**The Dahlia:** ...Pretty careless indeed, just like an old friend of mine.

> **大丽花：**…还真够不小心呢，很像一位老朋友。

*(Approach The Dahlia)*

**The Dahlia:** Looks like we're in luck. We've found one of their "contingencies."

> **大丽花：**看来我们运气不错，附近果然有他们的「备用手段」。

**The Dahlia:** Do you have memories like that? A moment in time you never want to forget, one that you find yourself reliving over and over again.

> **大丽花：**你有过类似的记忆吗？始终不愿忘却，无数次回想的一段时光。

**Firefly:** Of course... and more than just one.

> **流萤：**当然…而且不止一段。

**The Dahlia:** What a shame.

> **大丽花：**真可惜啊。

**Firefly:** ...?

> **流萤：**…？

> **大丽花：**别多心，我只是在模仿某些惹人厌烦的忆者。在他们看来，一段记忆被回想得越多，就越容易面目全非。

**The Dahlia:** Each time you recall a memory, you risk distorting its true face. That's how they justify the necessity of Light Cones. How tragic. They never understood the truth: The more eternal a thing, the more it stirs the desire to destroy it.

> **大丽花：**每次回忆，你都可能篡改它的面目。借此，他们强调着光锥的必要性。多么可悲啊，从未明白过，越是永恒的事物，越会引发毁灭的欲望。

**The Dahlia:** But they weren't wrong about the present situation. Even if Gopher Wood were to come in person, he'd need to bring a specific Light Cone. His own memories can no longer be trusted.

> **大丽花：**但具体到现在，他们倒是没说错。即使歌斐木亲自到来，也必须带上特定的光锥。他自己的记忆，已经不再可靠。

**The Dahlia:** That's exactly why we have a "contingency" plan right in front of us.

> **大丽花：**正是为了避免这一可能，我们眼前，才会存在「备用手段」。

**Firefly:** But I haven't seen anything since just now...

> **流萤：**可从刚才开始，我就什么都没看到……

**The Dahlia:** Oh... sorry.

> **大丽花：**噢…抱歉。

> **大丽花：**在这里面，想必存有某种「温和的刺激」，让他能在此时将记忆找回，以免遭到篡改。

**Firefly:** But for us, that's probably meaningless. Whatever triggers his memories would just seem bizarre and random to us.

> **流萤：**可这对我们来说，应该毫无意义。能让他触景生情的事物，在我们眼中，只会莫名其妙。

**Firefly:** Still, let's go take a look inside. We might pick up some clues at least.

> **流萤：**不过，还是进去看看吧，总归能多获得一些线索。

**The Dahlia:** That's right. Go ahead. Every action brings joy...

> **大丽花：**没错，请吧，一切行动都将带来喜悦……

> **大丽花：**还有「代价」。

### Expose the Dreammaster's buried crimes

Paradise shall fall into this world of sorrow

It will shatter in the light of day

How could anything golden

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Firefly:** How can this be...

> **流萤：**怎么会……

> **流萤：** AR…214…真的是你吗……

**Firefly:** ...

> **流萤：**……

**The Dahlia:** You... don't have to force yourself. I can take care of it.

> **大丽花：**你…不必勉强，我可以代劳。

> **流萤：**不，没关系……

**The Dahlia:** Was it her that you saw when infiltrating Penacony?

> **大丽花：**潜入匹诺康尼时，你见到的也是她吗？

**Firefly:** Yes, I saw her fighting off the Swarm. But it was just memoria, and it vanished quickly. Silver Wolf mentioned that Penacony had also faced a Swarm invasion before. I kept telling myself that she was just passing by and lending a helping hand.

> **流萤：**是，她正在清剿虫群。但那只是忆质，很快就消失了。银狼说过，匹诺康尼也曾遭遇过虫灾，我一直试着告诉自己，她只是在路过时伸出了援手。

**Firefly:** And after everything was over, she had already left safe and sound.

> **流萤：**在一切结束后，她已经安然无恙地离开了。

**The Dahlia:** So this time, do you still want to know the answer?

> **大丽花：**那么这一次，你还想知道答案吗？

**Firefly:** Would it be any different? I really want you to tell me... that this is not real. It's just memoria.

> **流萤：**会有什么不同吗？我很想听你告诉我…这也只是忆质而已，并非真实。

**Firefly:** But thinking about it calmly, nothing can change it. A memory... is ultimately something that's already happened.

> **流萤：**可冷静想想，那什么也无法改变，记忆…终究是已经发生过的事。

**The Dahlia:** ...You're more composed than I expected.

> **大丽花：**…你比我想象中镇定。

**Firefly:** I wish I wasn't like this.

> **流萤：**我宁可不是这样。

**Firefly:** She and I... we could be called friends. I wish I could just break down and cry for her, like any normal person would, to mourn her loss.

> **流萤：**我和她…应该能被称为朋友。我更希望为她大哭一场，像所有普通人那样，为她感到悲伤。

**Firefly:** But what surfaced in my mind was that one sentence...

> **流萤：**可浮现在我脑海里的，却是那样一句话——

**Firefly:** This is our predestined fate, and one day, I too shall meet the same end.

> **流萤：**这是我们早已注定的命运，总有一天，我也要迎来相同的结局。

**The Dahlia:** "One day."

> **大丽花：**「总有一天」。

**Firefly:** Hmm, not today. Though it's already too late, there are still other things I can do for her.

> **流萤：**嗯，不是今天。虽然为时已晚，但也有其他事，我能为她去做。

**The Dahlia:** Maybe you should calm down first. Unfortunately, this isn't memoria, but a real Glamoth Knight. Clearly, Penacony was her final destination.

> **大丽花：**也许你该先去静一静，很遗憾，这并非忆质，而是真正的格拉默铁骑。显而易见，匹诺康尼是她生命的最后一站。

**Firefly:** I'm surprised she made it to Penacony at all.

> **流萤：**她能够来到匹诺康尼，已经令我很惊讶了。

**The Dahlia:** Oh? The Glamoth Regulations? She broke those prohibitions?

> **大丽花：**哦？是「格拉默军规」？她违背过那些禁令？

**Firefly:** Worse than that, she was the first and only "deserter" of the Glamoth Iron Cavalry. The Empire hadn't fallen yet, but she didn't want to accept a fate of dying in battle, so she left on her own. She said... she wanted to choose how she would die.

> **流萤：**更严重，她是格拉默铁骑第一位，也是唯一一位「叛逃者」。那时帝国仍未覆灭，但她不想接受战死的命运，独自离开了。她说…想要自己选择如何死去。

**Firefly:** We had a brief farewell... but I didn't stop her, I couldn't... Leaving the Glamoth Empire itself would quickly drain her life away.

> **流萤：**我们有过一次短暂的告别…但我没有阻止她，我做不到。离开格拉默帝国，本身就会让她迅速失去生命。

**Firefly:** If we can't change the outcome, why should I stop her from making her own choice?

> **流萤：**既然我们无法改变结果，我又为什么，要阻止她选择过程呢？

**The Dahlia:** Quickly drain her life away... oh, Entropy Loss Syndrome?

> **大丽花：**迅速死去…噢，「失熵症」？

**Firefly:** Yes, just like me. "Leaving the cockpit without permission is strictly prohibited. Surviving knights must actively return to formation." These aren't just military regulations. They're the terms of our survival.

> **流萤：**嗯，就像我一样——「未经允许，严禁脱离驾驶舱；存活的骑士应主动归队」，这些不仅仅是军规，也是我们延续生命的条件。

**Firefly:** When not piloting the Fyrefly Type IV, I must stay in the cryosleep pod. Otherwise, my Entropy Loss Syndrome will only get worse.

> **流萤：**除了驾驶「火萤IV型」以外，我都必须呆在休眠舱里。否则，失熵症会进一步恶化。

**The Dahlia:** So, have you figured out the reason yet?

> **大丽花：**原因呢，现在你有眉目了吗？

**Firefly:** When (Trailblazer) and Kafka found me, I had just finished my duties as a Glamoth Knight...

> **流萤：**卡芙卡和开拓者找到我的那会儿，我刚刚结束了作为格拉默铁骑的……

**Firefly:** ...my final battle as a Glamoth Knight.

> **流萤：**…作为格拉默铁骑的最后一战。

**Firefly:** The Empire fell, and all restrictions vanished. For the first time, green flames ignited upon my body... and for the first time, I touched the true Path power that the Glamoth knights wield.

> **流萤：**帝国就此覆灭，一切限制都不复存在，绿色的火焰第一次在我身上燃起…而我也第一次，接触到了格拉默铁骑真正的命途力量。

**Firefly:** That's when I realized... Entropy Loss Syndrome is a death called living.

> **流萤：**那时我才明白——失熵症是名为「活着」的死亡。

**The Dahlia:** The Propagation? That's the true source of the Glamoth Iron Cavalry's power?

> **大丽花：**繁育？那才是格拉默铁骑的力量来源？

**Firefly:** Yes, I've seen it happen. When the Swarm King dies, the Swarm completely loses its vitality and falls into silence.

> **流萤：**是啊，我曾见过这样的情景——在归属的王虫死后，虫群会彻底失去活力，陷入沉寂。

**Firefly:** Just like Glamoth's Iron Cavalry, they weren't given a sense of self. The moment they stray from the "hive", they're already dead. The same goes for Entropy Loss Syndrome.

> **流萤：**和格拉默铁骑一样，它们没有被赋予自我，「离群」的那一刻，就已经死去。失熵症，也是如此。

**Firefly:** Could you help me take her away? I don't want to leave her here alone.

> **流萤：**能帮我带她离开吗？我不想让她独自留下。

**Firefly:** After all this is over, I want to give her a proper burial, just like any normal person.

> **流萤：**在一切结束后，我想将她好好安葬，像普通人那样。

**The Dahlia:** I'm happy to help.

> **大丽花：**乐意效劳。

**The Dahlia:** She wanted to escape her fate, but in the end, she died fighting against the Swarm in Penacony. The detestable thing about destiny is that it brooks no defiance.

> **大丽花：**她想要逃离命运，最终，却还是在匹诺康尼与虫群抗争至死。命运的可憎，正因它不容抗辩。

**The Dahlia:** Do you know her name?

> **大丽花：**你知道她的名字吗？

**Firefly:** When we parted, she had no name, just like me. But I hope... by the time she leaves this world, she will have found both a name and a purpose for her life.

> **流萤：**分别时，她还没有名字，就像曾经的我。但我希望…离开世界时，她已经找到了——无论名字，还是生存的理由。

**The Dahlia:** Why did Gopher Wood leave her here? No matter how much I think about it, everything he's scheming couldn't possibly be connected to the Propagation.

> **大丽花：**歌斐木将她留在这里，究竟是为了什么呢？他正在筹划的一切，无论怎么想，也无法与「繁育」产生联想。

**The Dahlia:** How deeply is your past intertwined with this place?

> **大丽花：**你的过去，又与这里产生了多深的关联？

**Firefly:** It's not clear yet, but obviously, she had multiple fatal wounds on her body... she definitely didn't die from entropy loss.

> **流萤：**现在还不清楚，但很明显，她身上有许多处足以致死的伤痕——她绝不是失熵而死。

**Firefly:** Before we left, Kafka told me: in her view, dreams are inherently a "convergence of the past." It's the same for everyone, no matter how much you want to avoid it, no matter what you're seeking, you can't avoid it. Perhaps she was right.

> **流萤：**出发前，卡芙卡对我说过：在她看来，梦原本就是「过去」的交汇，每个人都一样，无论你多么不想面对，也无论你想要得到什么，都无法绕开——或许她说得对。

**Firefly:** However, the Dreammaster's weakness has also given us an opportunity.

> **流萤：**不过，梦主的虚弱，也给我们带来了机会。

**The Dahlia:** Good, now then...

> **大丽花：**不错，接下来……

**The Dahlia:** With such a high-stakes gamble, he succeeded in provoking the Emanator's strike. The Family's carefully guarded secret was exposed, and you, caught in the eye of the storm, were pulled into the chaos.

## 228　The Moment Before My Death　我弥留之际

**Guileless Girl:** Brother, I don't understand... For a bird, does flying... also need a "why"?

> **沉郁的少年：**既然要由我们来决定，是否把它放归天空，那我们就得为此负责。

**Robin:** ...Until I returned once again for the Charmony Festival.

> **知更鸟：**…直到我为谐乐大典再次归来。

**Robin:** In the Twelve Hours converged by the Dreammaster, I developed symptoms of losing my voice, and the strength of the Harmony began to wane. Yet in Dreamflux Reef, I wasn't affected at all.

> **知更鸟：**在「梦主」融汇的十二时刻，我出现了失声的症状，「同谐」的力量也开始衰微。而在流梦礁，我却完全没有受到影响。

**Robin:** I believe that the Path he once pointed to might not have been named the Harmony.

> **知更鸟：**我想，那时他曾指向的「命途」，未必名为「同谐」。

**Robin:** That's why I gradually became incompatible with the sweet dream. In the past, the Dreammaster would harmonize for me, but when he was too busy to tend to me, I finally noticed something was wrong.

> **知更鸟：**因此我和美梦逐渐无法相容。过去是「梦主」为我进行调和，在他无暇他顾之时，我才终于感到异常。

**Firefly:** So, which Path did he choose to walk?

> **流萤：**那么，他走上了哪一条命途？

**Robin:** It's not clear yet. There aren't many clues about this, but I suspect it's the Order.

> **知更鸟：**尚不清楚，与此有关的线索不多。但我怀疑，是「秩序」。

**Firefly:** ...Thank you, Miss Robin. You've helped us clear up many of our doubts. However, regarding the Dreammaster's Path... we have different conjectures.

> **流萤：**…多谢，知更鸟小姐，你帮我们解开了许多困惑。但「梦主」倒向的命途…我们有不同的猜想。

**Robin:** ...What is it?

> **知更鸟：**…是什么？

**The Dahlia:** Come with me. It's time to see it with your own eyes.

### Leave the room

> *In their eyes, Dreammaster hasn't sided with the Order, yet the other Paths can't be connected in any way. The evidence they're about to present will likely far exceed my understanding.*

**The Dahlia:** Come with me. It's time to see it with your own eyes.

**Firefly:** ...Thank you, Miss Robin. You've helped us clear up many of our doubts. However, regarding the Dreammaster's Path... we have different conjectures.

**The Dahlia:** Here lies secrets that The Family tried to conceal, and a long, romantic tale. Sadly, most of it has nothing to do with us.

> **大丽花：**这里安葬着家族试图掩藏的秘密，和一段悠久而浪漫的故事。可惜，其中的大多与我们无关。

**Robin:** Breukelen Tiernan... I remember that name. A Galaxy Ranger worthy of admiration. When Penacony was plagued by the Swarm Disaster in the past, he was the one who fearlessly protected our sweet dream.

> **知更鸟：**博雷克林•铁尔南…我记得这个名字。一位值得敬佩的巡海游侠，历史上匹诺康尼遭受虫灾时，是他奋不顾身守护住了美梦。

**Firefly:** That's why we had to come here. We suspect that after turning away from The Family, the Dreammaster sided with the Propagation.

> **流萤：**这就是我们必须来这里的原因。我们怀疑，「梦主」在背离家族之后，倒向了「繁育」。

**Robin:** ...How could that be?

> **知更鸟：**…怎么可能？

**The Dahlia:** History as we know it may be long gone, but people's memories remain preserved in the Memory Zone...

> **大丽花：**所谓的历史早已逝去，但人们的记忆却沉积在忆域之中……

**The Dahlia:** If we can master the technique, we'll be able to pierce through time itself and salvage the past from chaos.

> **大丽花：**倘若能得其法，我们就能洞穿时间，将过往从混沌中打捞出来。

**Robin:** Is this... memoria?

> **知更鸟：**这是…忆质？

**Firefly:** ...

> **流萤：**……

**Firefly:** Every disaster brought by the Swarm was accompanied by countless sacrifices. Some names were remembered, but others... were not only forgotten, but betrayed.

> **流萤：**虫群引发的每一场灾难，都伴随着无数的牺牲者。一些名字留下了，但还有一些人…不但被遗忘，还遭到了背叛。

**AR-214:** What... are you doing... I'm not your enemy...

> **AR-214：**你…在干什么…我不是你的敌人……

**Dreammaster Gopher Wood:** Indeed. Our true foe is that abominable fate.

> **「梦主」歌斐木：**不错，你又或我，仇敌唯有那可憎命数。

**AR-214:** Then why... why...

> **AR-214：**那你又为什么…为什么……

**Dreammaster Gopher Wood:** Easy, child. Your struggle only adds to the pain.

> **「梦主」歌斐木：**冷静些吧，孩子，挣扎徒增苦痛。

**Dreammaster Gopher Wood:** The strike I just delivered — dishonorable though it may be — did not miss. Here and now, your death is certain.

> **「梦主」歌斐木：**我方才施予的一击，虽不光彩却绝无错失——今时此地，你已必死无疑。

**AR-214:** But I... I was clearly... helping fight against the Swarm here...

> **AR-214：**可我…我分明是在…帮这里对抗虫灾……

**Dreammaster Gopher Wood:** Indeed, as the Swarm ran rampant, a Glamoth Knight passing through joined the battle without asking for anything in return... Truly admirable.

> **「梦主」歌斐木：**不错，虫群肆虐横行，一位格拉默铁骑行经此地，不问回报投身战场，这实在值得钦佩。

**Dreammaster Gopher Wood:** But have you ever wondered why, right after that, the infestation only grew worse?

> **「梦主」歌斐木：**可你是否想过，为何偏偏在那之后，虫灾变本加厉？

**AR-214:** Could it be...

> **AR-214：**难道……

**Dreammaster Gopher Wood:** The Glamoth Knight, born to purge the Swarm, turned out to be a Pathstrider walking the Path of Propagation.

> **「梦主」歌斐木：**为清剿虫群而生的铁骑，竟是行于「繁育」的命途行者。

**Dreammaster Gopher Wood:** The sweet dream has never been tainted by the Propagation... You brought it here. And only your death can remove it.

> **「梦主」歌斐木：**美梦从未渗入「繁育」，是你将其带来此处。也唯有你的死亡，才能将其除去。

**AR-214:** How... can this... be...

> **AR-214：**怎么…会……

**AR-214:** I... can leave, and never... come back here...

> **AR-214：**但我…可以离开，再也…不回到这里…

**AR-214:** I can't die... I don't want to die...

> **AR-214：**我不能死…我不想死…我……

**Dreammaster Gopher Wood:** That's precisely why I said that strike wasn't honorable. I explored your secrets for the sake of the sweet dream, but striking you down? That came from selfish desires after glimpsing eternity.

> **「梦主」歌斐木：**正因如此，我才说那一击并不光彩。探究你的秘密，是为了美梦；将你击落，却出于窥见永恒后的私心。

**Dreammaster Gopher Wood:** "11:45█Paradise shall fall into this world of sorrow, and once dawn breaks, it will shatter in the light of day. How could anything golden abide in glory forever?"

> **「梦主」歌斐木：**「11：45█乐园终将跌入愁苦人世，黎明一旦升起，便要坠毁于白昼。凡是金的，怎可能光华长留？」

**Dreammaster Gopher Wood:** You shall become my darkest crime, and the beginning of a great undertaking.

> **「梦主」歌斐木：**你将成为我最为阴暗的一桩罪行，也将成为一项伟业的起始。

**Dreammaster Gopher Wood:** Your remains shall become the foundation for "Eternal Paradise."

> **「梦主」歌斐木：**我将以你的残躯，为「永恒乐园」奠基。

**The Dahlia:** You're quite familiar with memoria, so you should be able to tell whether this memory is fabricated or not.

> **大丽花：**你对忆质相当熟悉，这段记忆是否出于捏造，你应该能够甄别。

**Robin:** Laying the foundation for "Eternal Paradise?" This...

> **知更鸟：**为「永恒乐园」奠基？这……

**Firefly:** At that time, she was trying to escape her own destiny.

> **流萤：**那时她正在试图逃离自己的命运。

**Firefly:** But when the Swarm was discovered on Penacony, she didn't just stand idly by. Even though she died while fighting against the Swarm, it wasn't destiny catching up with her again.

> **流萤：**但在匹诺康尼发现虫群时，她没有袖手旁观。即便她仍在抗争虫群时死去，但那不是命运再次追上了她。

**Firefly:** It was her own choice, because like any ordinary person, she had a conscience and knew compassion.

> **流萤：**而是她自己的选择，因为她和普通人一样，拥有良知，懂得怜悯。

**Robin:** ...Is she your friend?

> **知更鸟：**…她是你的朋友吗？

**Firefly:** Yes... And in many moments, looking at her is like seeing myself.

> **流萤：**是，甚至很多时候…像是我自己。

**Robin:** I know it won't undo what's been done, but... I'm sorry. No matter what purpose the Dreammaster has, it doesn't give him the right to sacrifice anyone.

> **知更鸟：**我知道这样于事无补，但…实在抱歉。无论「梦主」出于什么目的，也没有让任何人牺牲的权利。

**Robin:** I promise you, when all of this is over, regardless of the outcome, I'll tell everyone about her deeds. She deserves to be remembered by the sweet dream.

> **知更鸟：**我向你承诺，在一切结束之后，无论结果如何，我会把她的作为告诉人们——她应当被美梦铭记。

**Firefly:** Thank you.

> **流萤：**谢谢。

**Robin:** But there's one thing I don't understand. Even if the Dreammaster is after the power of the Propagation, there are plenty of Swarms left in the cosmos. Why resort to killing?

> **知更鸟：**可有件事，我不明白。就算「梦主」意在「繁育」的力量，银河中不乏残存的虫群。为何非得痛下杀手？

**The Dahlia:** That's exactly why we came to find you.

> **大丽花：**这就是我们来找你的原因。

**The Dahlia:** With the Charmony Festival about to begin, it can't be a mere coincidence that the Dreammaster is pushing forward with his various machinations now, and you were chosen to perform for the Harmony. Whether you're aware of it or not, you must be absolutely crucial to his plans.

> **大丽花：**谐乐大典开幕在即，无论「梦主」抱有什么目的，他在此时推进种种行动，不可能只是巧合，而你被他选中，将要为「同谐」献唱。无论是否知情，你都一定极为关键。

**Robin:** I see...

> **知更鸟：**原来如此……

**Robin:** You want me to become the "conduit" to retrieve the "past" from the Memory Zone? What exactly do I need to do?

> **知更鸟：**你们想让我成为「引子」，从忆域中打捞「过去」？我需要为此做些什么？

**The Dahlia:** I'll need to temporarily take a piece of your memory. By letting it stir up the Memory Zone, I can follow the flow of memoria and burn a path through, retrieving scenes from the past once again.

> **大丽花：**我要暂时取走你的一段记忆。使它搅动忆域，我便能顺着忆质的流动，灼烧出一条通路，再次打捞出过去的情景。

**The Dahlia:** Whether I succeed or not, I'll return your memories to you safe and sound.

> **大丽花：**无论成功与否，你的记忆我都会妥善奉还。

**Robin:** Let a Cremator access my memories?

> **知更鸟：**让一名焚化工接触我的记忆？

**Firefly:** I also think it's too much to ask. If you deem it necessary, you can use "Tuning" to leave a safeguard in our minds, preventing any ulterior motives.

> **流萤：**我也觉得强人所难——如果你认为有必要，可以借助「调律」，在我们的意识中留下保险，确保我们不会另有企图。

**Robin:** ...

> **知更鸟：**……

**Robin:** There's no need. Let's get started.

> **知更鸟：**不必了，直接开始吧。

**Robin:** "May the harm that befalls you never lead to your downfall." This is a blessing that has been passed down through The Family for generations. I don't want all trust to be built upon mutual threats.

> **知更鸟：**「若你受到伤害，愿那伤害不致使你堕落」——这是在家族中流传已久的祝福。我不希望所有信任，都建立于相互威胁之上。

**The Dahlia:** So this is what star power looks like... you've even made me a little star-struck.

> **大丽花：**这就是大明星的气度么，你让我也产生了一些向往呢。

**The Dahlia:** Alright. Looks like we got lucky.

> **大丽花：**好了——运气不错。

**The Dahlia:** Oh? What's this? A piece of worm-eaten oak?

> **大丽花：**哦？这是什么？一节已被蛀朽的橡木？

**Firefly:** Found something new?

> **流萤：**有新发现吗？

**The Dahlia:** This time, I'm afraid we've uncovered quite a "disaster." Let's go see it with our own eyes.

> **大丽花：**这回，我们恐怕揭开了一场「灾难」呢。亲眼去看看吧。

**Black Swan:** Nothing but lies, my dear.

> **黑天鹅：**谎话连篇呢，亲爱的。

**Black Swan:** Hiding behind the title of a Memokeeper... Tell me, how long have you been lying to them?

> **黑天鹅：**凭借忆者的身份，你究竟蒙骗了她们多久？

**The Dahlia:** You really seem determined to savor those unforgettable moments, don't you? Be smart and take a hint. I don't have time to entertain you right now.

> **大丽花：**那段刻骨铭心的时光，你像是非要享受不可呢。识趣些，我现在没空招待你。

**Black Swan:** Oh, because you're busy... pretending to be a Stellaron Hunter?

> **黑天鹅：**哦，因为你正忙着…假装成一位「星核猎手」？

**Firefly:** ...Who are you talking about?

> **流萤：**…你指的是谁？

**Black Swan:** Quite the clever move there. Miss Firefly... if you're willing to take my advice, why not make a trip back to reality? Ask that Silver Wolf lady carefully who your true companions really are.

> **黑天鹅：**当真是好手段。流萤女士…若你愿意听听我的建议，不妨回现实一趟吧。仔细问问那位银狼女士，谁才是你真正的同伴。

**Firefly:** How did you...

> **流萤：**你怎么……

**The Dahlia:** Alright, you... still can't help sticking your nose into everything, huh.

> **大丽花：**好了，你呀…还是那么喜欢多管闲事。

**Robin:** Who... exactly are you people?

> **知更鸟：**你们…究竟是什么人？

**Black Swan:** She's... just a Cremator who manipulated memories and pretended to be a Stellaron Hunter's companion. She fooled all of you.

> **黑天鹅：**她么…只是一个趁机摆弄记忆，假装成星核猎手同伴的焚化工。你们都被她骗过了。

**Robin:** What about Miss Firefly? Is she alright?

> **知更鸟：**流萤小姐呢？她没事吧？

**Black Swan:** She might get lost in the Memory Zone's turbulence, but don't worry, she's quite something. She'll only be stuck there temporarily.

> **黑天鹅：**或许会迷失在忆域的乱流中，别担心，她大有来头，只会被暂时困住而已。

**Black Swan:** I'm here to bring you back to the Oak Family. Mr. Sunday is worried about your safety. He tasked me with finding you in the dreamscape.

> **黑天鹅：**我是来带你回到橡木家系的——星期日先生很担心你的安危，他委托我在梦境中找到你。

**Black Swan:** As for this Dahlia, she cannot be trusted. To extract specific history from the Memory Zone through just one memory... While there might be Memokeepers capable of such feats, they certainly wouldn't be mere Pathstriders.

> **黑天鹅：**至于这位大丽花，她绝不可信。只通过一段记忆，就从忆域中打捞出特定的历史…能做到这一点的忆者或许存在，但绝不会只是命途行者。

**The Dahlia:** Heh, seems you've been watching for quite a while, yet you only show up "fashionably late" when we're about to make progress... Who's the untrustworthy one here, I wonder?

> **大丽花：**呵，看来你窥视了很久嘛，但在我们即将取得进展时，你才「姗姗来迟」——不可信的，究竟是谁呢？

**Black Swan:** Save your excuses. I bring a message from Mr. Sunday that will prove my identity.

> **黑天鹅：**不必狡辩了。我带来了星期日先生的口信，足以证明我的身份。

**The Dahlia:** ...Just a message? Don't you find that a bit ridiculous?

> **大丽花：**…一句口信？你不觉得有些可笑吗？

**Black Swan:** Let's give it a try. It's only two words...

> **黑天鹅：**试试看就知道了，也只有三个字而已——

**Black Swan:** "Charmony Dove."

> **黑天鹅：**「谐乐鸽」。

**Robin:** ...!?

> **知更鸟：**…？！

**Black Swan:** I don't know the details, but Mr. Sunday said you would understand.

> **黑天鹅：**我不知内情，星期日先生说，你会明白的。

**Robin:** ...Indeed, unless it's an extreme situation, this is enough to earn my trust.

> **知更鸟：**…的确，除非是极端情况，这足以让我产生信任。

**The Dahlia:** The IPC, Astral Express... and now you're even taking assignments from The Family? My, aren't you "popular."

> **大丽花：**公司，星穹列车…现在你连家族的委托也接下了？「人缘」真不错。

**The Dahlia:** ...But you just had to choose the path of your own demise?

> **大丽花：**…可你就非要自寻死路？

**The Dahlia:** Back then, I not only kept Lady Acheron's secret, but also left you with your life intact.

> **大丽花：**那时，我非但留下了黄泉女士的秘密，还就此离开，放了你一条生路。

**The Dahlia:** What, you're that eager to... "rekindle our past"?

> **大丽花：**怎么，你就这么希望…和我「再续前缘」？

**Black Swan:** Well, you are The Dahlia after all. I have to keep an eye on what kind of trouble you're going to stir up next.

> **黑天鹅：**谁让你是大丽花呢？我总要看看，你又要闹出什么乱子。

**Black Swan:** I've been questioning this for a long time... How could Destiny's Slave possibly accept someone like you, whose very nature is betrayal?

> **黑天鹅：**我为此疑问了很久…「命运的奴隶」，怎么可能会接纳你，一个天性为背叛的人。

**Robin:** And what's the deal with the Stellaron Hunter?

> **知更鸟：**星核猎手呢，那又是怎么回事？

**Black Swan:** Let's get somewhere safe first, then we can talk. She's too dangerous for us to let our guard down.

> **黑天鹅：**先回安全的地方去吧，之后再慢慢聊——她太危险了，不该掉以轻心。

**Robin:** ...Many thanks.

> **知更鸟：**…多谢。

**Black Swan:** You've gotten more "composed," or have you just gotten slower?

> **黑天鹅：**「稳重」了不少呢，还是你的反应变慢了？

**Black Swan:** She's already left this area. There's no way you can catch up to her now.

> **黑天鹅：**她已经离开这片区域了，你不可能再追得上。

**The Dahlia:** ...Catch?

> **大丽花：**…追？

**The Dahlia:** ...Why would I chase after her? Arrogant swan... if I didn't take her memories just now, then what exactly did I do to her?

> **大丽花：**…我为什么要去追她？自负的天鹅，如果我刚才没有取走她的记忆，那我究竟对她做了些什么呢？

**Black Swan:** ...?

> **黑天鹅：**…？

**The Dahlia:** I might as well tell you. I've planted a spark of the Cremators' fire in her memories.

> **大丽花：**告诉你也无妨，我在她的记忆中，留下了一簇焚化工的火苗。

**The Dahlia:** It will spread silently, and in an instant, it will burn away all her memories of seeing me. She'll believe she was never here.

> **大丽花：**它将悄无声息地蔓延，立刻，就会将她见到我的记忆全部焚毁。她会认为自己从未来过这里。

**The Dahlia:** The suspicions that have already taken root in her mind will also gradually fade away. No matter what she has discovered, the moment she attends the Charmony Festival, she'll revert back to that... good child who was born to walk on the Order.

> **大丽花：**她已然生出的疑心，也将慢慢不复存在——无论她已经查到了什么，登上谐乐大典的那一刻，她将变回那个…生来行于「秩序」的好孩子。

**The Dahlia:** You're the one who's run out of time.

> **大丽花：**来不及的人，是你呀。

**Black Swan:** I see. Not a member of the Ever-Flame Mansion, and definitely not a Stellaron Hunter... Your true ally is the Dreammaster?

> **黑天鹅：**原来如此。不是永火官邸的一员，也绝非星核猎手，你真正的盟友，是「梦主」本人？

**The Dahlia:** Aren't you clever? How about a little "reward" for that?

> **大丽花：**真聪明呢，给你一点「奖励」，如何？

**Black Swan:** Cut the act. Even with your usual reckless methods, you can't handle me alone.

> **黑天鹅：**不必故作姿态了。就算你行事一向不顾代价，也没办法独自对付我。

**The Dahlia:** You're right, I can't do it alone... But I rarely feel lonely anyway.

> **大丽花：**说得对，独自一人，我的确做不到——可我很少会感到孤单呢。

"11:30█ Repent and return to righteousness. Though you soar like an eagle and make your nest among the stars, I will bring you down from there."

**The Dahlia:** Now, can you still keep your cool?

> **大丽花：**现在，你还能强装镇定吗？

**Black Swan:** He actually gave you strength through the "divine law"? The Dreammaster would go this far for an ally?

> **黑天鹅：**他通过「律令」向你分享了力量？「梦主」竟会为盟友做到这种程度？

**The Dahlia:** What can I say? I'm just that trustworthy! If he hadn't done that, how could my control over memoria far exceed that of ordinary Memokeepers, let alone... allow me to tamper with a Stellaron Hunter's memories?

> **大丽花：**谁让我是那样值得信赖呢？如果他不这么做，我对忆质的掌控，又怎能远远超过寻常忆者，更别说…能篡改一名星核猎手的记忆了。

**Black Swan:** Unfortunately, you lost your composure in your euphoria. In the dreamscape, I might not be your match. But if I want to leave, you can't keep me here.

> **黑天鹅：**可惜，你还是因狂喜而失态了。在梦境中，我暂且不是你的对手。但我想走，你也留不住。

**The Dahlia:** Harsh words... Want to test that theory? It's easy to throw away a flower, but the fragrance lingering on your hands won't fade so easily. I can be quite persistent, you know.

> **大丽花：**这话真够绝情啊…想试试看吗？把一朵花丢掉容易，但手上的花香，可不会那么容易散去——我很缠人的。

**The Dahlia:** Even if we rekindle what once was, I remain unchanged.

> **大丽花：**即便是再续前缘，我也从未改变。

**The Dahlia:** Run faster, I love watching people resist, love watching them flee. Let's savor this moment a bit longer. After all, for us, this will be an incredibly crucial moment.

> **大丽花：**跑快点吧，我喜欢看别人反抗，也喜欢看别人逃跑，我们得多享受一会儿，毕竟对我们来说，这会是无比重要的一刻。

**The Dahlia:** No matter where you run, I will always find you.

> **大丽花：**无论逃去哪里，我都会找到你的。

*(Cutscene begins)*

**The Dahlia:** I have the numbers of advantage. You have no chance of winning.

> **大丽花：**眼下可是「以多欺少」，你没有胜算。

**Black Swan:** I don't want to use a Cremators tricks... Not that i can't.

> **黑天鹅：**「焚化工」的伎俩——我只是不想，而非不能。

**The Dahlia:** I knew it. Your ever-shifting morals...

> **大丽花：**果然，你多变的道德底线——

**The Dahlia:** Never changed.

> **大丽花：**倒是从未改变。

*(Cutscene ends)*

The Dahlia's memories are slowly emerging...

In an enigmatic dream you had yet to enter, The Dahlia wanted to show you her true appearance...

**Black Swan:** ...Where do you think you're going? After putting on such an elaborate show, won't you stay for a little chat?

> **黑天鹅：**…要去哪儿啊？费尽心机，演了这么一出好戏，不过来聊聊吗？

### Chat with Black Swan about the situation

> *The Dreammaster's containment mechanism was completely burned away by Black Swan, yet their old rapport remained intact, though the origin of such rapport wasn't exactly worth reminiscing about. It's time to tell Black Swan the whole truth.*

*(Talk to Black Swan)*

**Black Swan:** The most trustworthy thing about you is that you're "never to be trusted," darling. And that's one thing that's never changed about you.

> **黑天鹅：**你最值得信任的一点，就是你「永远不该被信任」，亲爱的。这一点，你也从未改变呢。

**The Dahlia:** When did you figure it out... that the "divine law" used to share power with me was also the shackles he placed upon me?

> **大丽花：**什么时候看出来的——歌斐木分享力量的「律令」，也是他施予我的禁制。

**Black Swan:** The moment you admitted to being the Dreammaster's ally, I began to suspect your true intentions. Until now, you've betrayed every single faction you've joined, without exception.

> **黑天鹅：**你承认自己是梦主盟友的那一刻，我就开始怀疑你真正的用意了。迄今为止，你背叛过自己加入的每一方势力，从无例外。

**Black Swan:** Besides... after not seeing each other for so long, and now having such an overwhelming advantage over me, you'd do everything in your power to stop me even if I had the slightest chance to escape.

> **黑天鹅：**况且…许久不见，又在我面前有了压倒性的优势，哪怕我仅有半分逃离的机会，你也会竭力阻止。

**Black Swan:** The openings you deliberately left during our clash weren't exactly subtle. Rather than toying with me, you were... leaving gaps everywhere for me to "cremate memoria."

> **黑天鹅：**方才交手时，你刻意留下的破绽可不怎么高明。与其说是在取乐，不如说…处处给我留下「焚化忆质」的空隙。

**The Dahlia:** Oh, really? I'd love to drop the act and give you a proper taste of torment. But alas, as long as the constraints on me remain, I'm not my own master. I can't do anything that would harm Gopher Wood, nor can I reveal his secrets.

> **大丽花：**是吗？我倒很想假戏真做，好好折磨你一番呢。可惜，只要禁制尚存，我就身不由己。不能作出任何不利于歌斐木的举止，也无法透露他的秘密。

**The Dahlia:** Good thing we've always had this kind of tacit understanding... Always trusting in each other's "capriciousness."

> **大丽花：**还好我们一直这么默契，始终相信对方的「反复无常」。

**Black Swan:** I wish that wasn't the case. I'd rather not draw the Garden's attention because of my association with you.

> **黑天鹅：**我更希望没这回事，我可不想因为与你的交集，引起忆庭的注意。

**The Dahlia:** Oh? Back to your old tricks again?

> **大丽花：**哦？你重操旧业了？

**Black Swan:** ...It's an epiphany, dear. Something you'll never understand. Let's talk about what's really important here: what are you hoping to get from the Dreammaster that made you willing to submit to his control?

> **黑天鹅：**…是幡然醒悟，亲爱的，你永远不会懂的。还是谈谈眼下的正事吧，你不惜受制于梦主，是想从他身上得到什么？

**Black Swan:** After putting on such an elaborate show, using me to regain your freedom... Which kind of memories are you trying to steal from the sweet dream?

> **黑天鹅：**演了这样一出戏，借我之手重获自由，又是为了从美梦中，窃取哪一种记忆？

**The Dahlia:** Why of course... the Destruction's.

> **大丽花：**当然是…「毁灭」。

**Black Swan:** ...Stop joking around. You abandoned that Path long ago.

> **黑天鹅：**…别再开玩笑了，你早就抛弃这条命途了。

**The Dahlia:** You're still as charming as ever.

> **大丽花：**你还是那么讨人喜欢。

**The Dahlia:** So, let me show you a glimpse of the past. Just this once, hmm?

> **大丽花：**那么，我来为你展示一段往事吧——仅此一次哦？

**The Dahlia:** Weren't you really interested in the Ever-Flame Mansion?

> **大丽花：**对于永火官邸，你不是很感兴趣么？

**Black Swan:** So you were the one who erased the memories from that music box after all.

> **黑天鹅：**那只八音盒中的记忆，果然是你亲手抹去的。

**The Dahlia:** That's right. I kept only this scene as a memento. I wanted to remember how I felt at that moment.

> **大丽花：**没错，只留下这一幕作为纪念——我想记住那一刻的心情。

**The Dahlia:** And... look closer. It's not just the memories. The Ever-Flame Mansion itself has ceased to exist.

> **大丽花：**还有…看仔细些，不光是记忆——永火官邸本身也不复存在了。

**Black Swan:** Those claw marks... You've long since strayed from that Path, yet you can still wield the power of the Destruction?

> **黑天鹅：**那些爪痕…早已叛离那条命途，你却仍能使用「毁灭」的力量？

**The Dahlia:** Isn't it the same right now? I've betrayed the Garden of Recollection, yet I can still show you the memories.

> **大丽花：**此时此刻，不也一样？我也叛离过流光忆庭，却还是能为你展示记忆。

**The Dahlia:** If you have time, I can... help you savor the memory of our first encounter.

> **大丽花：**有时间的话，我也可以…帮你回味下我们的第一次相遇。

**Black Swan:** Go ahead and savor it on your own. I want to put it behind me, the sooner, the better.

> **黑天鹅：**还是自己品味吧，我只希望自己尽快忘记。

**Black Swan:** But can't you tell me something I don't already know? You betrayed the Ever-Flame Mansion... Is that really supposed to be surprising?

> **黑天鹅：**不过，你就不能讲述些我不知情的往事吗？你背叛了永火官邸…这难道还值得意外？

**The Dahlia:** Destruction is a glorious moment. As Duke Inferno's "daughter," I will see his last wishes carried out to perfection, even if my role as such is only temporary.

> **大丽花：**毁灭是壮丽的一瞬——作为冥火大公的「女儿」，我当然要让他的遗愿尽善尽美，就算只是暂时的身份。

**The Dahlia:** And besides... who said I betrayed them back then? I came here to fulfill Duke Inferno's last wishes.

> **大丽花：**还有…谁说我在那时就背叛了他们？我来到这里，就是为了完成冥火大公的遗愿。

**The Dahlia:** This was his command to me: "Make the nights of Penacony crumble and wither, leave nought but a tomb of Remembrance."

> **大丽花：**这是他向我下达的命令——「让匹诺康尼的午夜枯萎凋零，只留下记忆的坟茔。」

**The Dahlia:** In other words, everything on Penacony related to the Remembrance was my target for destruction. But this was already a world of memoria. How could I possibly accomplish this without any help?

> **大丽花：**换句话说，匹诺康尼与「记忆」有关的一切，都是我应当毁灭的目标。但这本就是忆质的世界，没有帮手，我怎么可能做得到？

**The Dahlia:** Working with the Dreammaster may be like dancing with the devil, but I have no better choice.

> **大丽花：**与「梦主」的合作，虽是与虎谋皮，却也没有更好的选择。

**The Dahlia:** Oh yes, everything tied to the Remembrance includes you, of course. Grateful? You should be. My whim is the only reason you're still alive.

> **大丽花：**对了，与「记忆」有关的一切，当然也包括你。庆幸吗？多亏我的善变，你才免于一死。

**Black Swan:** I won't be grateful for that... You couldn't have done it anyway. Filial piety has never been one of your strong suits, so drop the act.

> **黑天鹅：**我可不会承这份情，你原本就做不到。孝心也不像是你会有的品质，别兜圈子了。

**The Dahlia:** That's exactly why I'm saying... you only think you understand me. Think about it carefully. As a Stellaron Hunter, didn't I work my hardest to help Firefly get closer to the truth?

> **大丽花：**所以我才说…你只是自以为懂我。仔细想想吧，作为星核猎手时，我难道没有尽心尽力，让流萤逐步接近真相？

**The Dahlia:** As an ally of the Dreammaster, I also helped monitor the dreamscape, and when a hunter infiltrated Penacony, I seized the chance to pose as her companion and trapped her in the Memory Zone.

> **大丽花：**作为梦主的盟友时，我也协助他监视梦境，在一名猎手潜入匹诺康尼时，借机伪装成她的同伴，将她困在了忆域当中。

**The Dahlia:** As for Robin, whom he chose as his songstress, I burned away her doubts too, leaving nothing to chance.

> **大丽花：**被他选为献唱者的知更鸟，我也焚去了她的疑心，确保万无一失。

**The Dahlia:** Like the Destruction, "betrayal" is but a fleeting moment. And before it came, whether with Duke Inferno, Gopher Wood, or Firefly... I wasn't pretending. I truly, wholeheartedly "became" their companion.

> **大丽花：**如同毁灭那般，「背叛」也仅是一瞬。而在它到来之前，无论冥火大公、歌斐木，还是流萤…我都不是假装，而是发自真心，暂时「成为」了他们的同伴。

**Black Swan:** What about Robin? Those sparks that burned through memories... don't you think that was a bit too much?

> **黑天鹅：**那知更鸟呢？那簇焚烧记忆的火花，你就不觉得太过吗？

**Sampo (?):** ...Oh my, I thought I could fool your eyes at the edge of the Dreamscape. But I guess there's no hiding from you on your home turf.

> **桑博（？）：**…哎呀，本以为在梦境边缘，能瞒过你的眼睛的。看来主场就是主场，哪儿都躲不掉。

**Sampo (?):** What, you want to settle the score with me for messing with your people? Why don't we sit down and have a chat? You seem like a patient one to me.

> **桑博（？）：**怎么，动了你的人，要找我算账？要不先坐下来谈谈呢，我看你也挺有耐心的。

**Sampo (?):** So, tell me... what exactly do you want to take from me?

> **桑博（？）：**所以，说说吧，你想从我这里…拿走什么？

**The Dahlia:** Don't worry, I only went with the flow... Suppressing her power a little to make it easier for you.

> **大丽花：**放心吧，我也只是顺水推舟，稍稍压制了她的力量，让你能这么容易就得手。

**The Dahlia:** I trust... you're someone who knows how to return a favor, right?

> **大丽花：**我相信…你一定是个知恩图报的人，对吧？

**The Dahlia:** There's this thing called the "Script." Now that, I find quite intriguing... Stellaron Hunter collaborator.

> **大丽花：**有个叫做「剧本」的东西，我倒是很有兴致——星核猎手的合作者。

**The Dahlia:** Soon, Robin will understand the true purpose of those sparks, and thank me for it.

> **大丽花：**很快，知更鸟就会知道那簇火花真正的用意，并因此感谢我。

**The Dahlia:** I can hardly wait. Even though I'm not a Stellaron Hunter, this time... I might have a chance to "betray destiny," too.

> **大丽花：**真令人迫不及待。即使并非星核猎手，这一次…我或许也有机会「叛离命运」呢。

**The Dahlia:** So stop thinking you've figured me out. I don't want to miss out on any possibility life has to offer.

> **大丽花：**所以，别再自以为了解我了——我呀，只是不想错过活着的任何一种可能。

**The Dahlia:** Betrayal isn't in my nature. "Greed" is.

> **大丽花：**我的天性不是背叛，而是「贪心」。

**Black Swan:** Darling, are you fatally wounded, or terminally ill? Unless you're on your deathbed, I don't believe you're actually being this honest.

> **黑天鹅：**亲爱的，你是受了致命伤，还是得了绝症？不是弥留之际，我不相信你会如此坦诚。

**The Dahlia:** I can make an exception for you. But most people? I certainly don't want them to remember.

> **大丽花：**对你可以网开一面——大多数人，我当然不希望他们记得。

**The Dahlia:** Sometimes, I resent memories... Because some people don't deserve to witness my radiant bloom again in their memories.

> **大丽花：**我有时会憎恨记忆，本就是因为…有些人不配在回忆中，再次欣赏我绽放时的身姿。

**The Dahlia:** And besides, you're no better when it comes to greed. On my deathbed? I suppose that's not entirely wrong... I was hoping a little honesty could talk you into "dying with me."

> **大丽花：**况且，在贪心这方面，你也不遑多让。弥留之际？也算是没说错…我想用这份坦诚，说动你一同「寻死」。

**Black Swan:** No, thank you.

> **黑天鹅：**容我拒绝。

**The Dahlia:** Too late. You're already caught up in this secret. Come on, just a few steps more. Even if I tried to turn you away, you wouldn't want to.

> **大丽花：**太迟啦——你已经身处秘密之中。来吧，只需要随便走几步，就算我赶你离开，你也不会愿意的。

### Uncover the secrets of this place

> *The past is behind us. Now, it's time for Black Swan to see it with her own eyes.*

**Black Swan:** I see now... "A piece of worm-eaten oak"... You weren't just making that up.

> **黑天鹅：**原来如此，「一节已被蛀朽的橡木」…那时你并非信口胡言。

**The Dahlia:** Oak wood is prone to "insect infestations." Nothing surprising about that.

> **大丽花：**橡木本就容易「生虫」，没什么好意外的。

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Black Swan:** It's him...

> **黑天鹅：**是他……

*(Approach Aventurine)*

**Aventurine:** Just look at this place. It shouldn't be this lively here. Ladies, which one of us would you say is the uninvited guest?

> **砂金：**瞧这儿的氛围，可不该热闹成这样啊。两位女士——我们哪一边才算是不速之客？

**Black Swan:** Mr. Aventurine, what are you doing here?

> **黑天鹅：**砂金先生，你怎么会在这里？

**Aventurine:** The game's over, and I scored a big win.

> **砂金：**赌局结束了，我赢得了一场大胜。

**Black Swan:** Oh? What did you get out of it?

> **黑天鹅：**哦？有何收获？

**Aventurine:** The Nihility Emanator cut me, and I died in the most spectacular fashion.

> **砂金：**「虚无」的令使给了我一刀，我死得无比盛大。

**The Dahlia:** Oh, speaking of which, you must know exactly how that feels? My dear lady who loves to dance?

> **大丽花：**哦，说到这里，你应该也感同身受？这位热衷于共舞的女士？

**Black Swan:** Still better than having no invites at all, my dear helpful lady.

> **黑天鹅：**总比没人邀请更好，这位热心助人的女士。

**Aventurine:** Ha, you two are... really something special.

> **砂金：**哈，两位还真是…情深意重。

**The Dahlia:** You're quite interesting, Mr. Aventurine. However...

> **大丽花：**你倒是很有意思，砂金先生，不过……

**The Dahlia:** You weren't supposed to find your way here. Unless you can give me a reason, I'll indulge you... By letting you die once more.

> **大丽花：**你不该找到这来——给我一个理由，否则我要投其所好，请你再死一次了。

**Aventurine:** That confident, huh?

> **砂金：**就这么有信心？

**The Dahlia:** You haven't hidden your weakness well. Even though you're one of the Ten Stonehearts, right now, you'll be easy enough to handle.

> **大丽花：**你没隐藏好自己的虚弱。虽是「石心十人」，此时此刻，料理起来却不难。

**Aventurine:** I wouldn't be so sure. Since you know who I am, you should know better than to gamble. Consider this warning a welcome gift from a friend...

> **砂金：**这可未必。既然你知道我，就别试着赌一把了。这句忠告，算是我给朋友的见面礼——

**Aventurine:** So long as my "trump card" remains unplayed, none will dare see the hand through to the end.

> **砂金：**只要我「底牌未出」，没有一个人敢跟注到底。

**The Dahlia:** Too bad you've used up all your luck for today. I've always found losing more interesting than winning. What's the harm in giving it a try?

> **大丽花：**很可惜，你今天的好运用完了。比起赢，我一直觉得输更有趣，试试又怎样？

**Black Swan:** Hold up, no need to rush into making enemies.

> **黑天鹅：**先停手吧，不必急于树敌。

**Black Swan:** Mr. Aventurine, congratulations on uncovering the truth behind "Death", but from what I know, you should be at Dreamflux Reef instead.

> **黑天鹅：**砂金先生，恭喜你揭开了「死亡」的真相，但就我所知，你更应该出现在流梦礁。

**Aventurine:** Hah, after taking a solid hit from the Emanator's blade, I never got the chance to enjoy a good nap.

> **砂金：**哈，结结实实挨了令使一刀，我可没机会享受沉眠。

**Aventurine:** Thus I sank into the chaotic Memory Zone, and then, my "desire" led me here.

> **砂金：**我因此沉入了混乱的忆域，此后，我的「渴求」，将我带至此地。

**Black Swan:** ...?

> **黑天鹅：**…？

**Aventurine:** Hah, as I thought. You two haven't noticed. The answer was right under your noses this whole time. You can traverse through memoria without relying on "roads," which is exactly why you missed it.

> **砂金：**哈，两位果然没意识到——灯下黑，说得就是现在。你们穿越忆质，不需要依赖「道路」，反而没能发觉。

**Aventurine:** Ladies, you didn't think the IPC would enter Penacony unprepared, did you?

> **砂金：**两位女士不会以为，公司在入局匹诺康尼前，毫无准备吧？

**Aventurine:** Backed by a most attractive package, I quietly assembled a team to research the Memory Zone. And I only asked for one thing in return: How to escape alive if I accidentally got lost in Penacony's memoria.

> **砂金：**凭借最具吸引力的酬劳，我暗中组建了一支研究忆域的团队。而我索取的成果只有一个——若我意外迷失在匹诺康尼的忆质中，该如何逃得生天。

**Aventurine:** To be honest, they didn't contribute a thing, but they did help me discover a certain "legacy"...

> **砂金：**老实说，他们没作出一点贡献，但却为我发现了一件「遗产」——

**Aventurine:** Razalina Jane Estella, the former undertaking once belonging to this Nameless.

> **砂金：**拉扎莉娜•简•艾丝黛拉，这位无名客曾经的事业。

**Aventurine:** She once boarded the Astral Express as an outstanding Surveyor. Years ago, in pursuit of the Primordial Dreamscape's secrets, she ventured alone into the Memory Zone and was never seen again.

> **砂金：**她曾登上星穹列车，是位出色的测绘师。多年前，为了探明原初梦境的秘密，她只身一人进入忆域，从此不知所踪。

**Aventurine:** Everyone believed she had perished, taking the mysteries of the Dreamscape to her grave. But that wasn't the truth.

> **砂金：**所有人都认为，她已然遇难，至死没能解开梦境之谜。可这并非真相。

**Aventurine:** Before departing from this world, she single-handedly forged the "pathway of memoria."

> **砂金：**在离开人世之前，她以一己之力，打造出了「忆质的航路」。

**Black Swan:** Mr. Aventurine, do you even realize what you're saying?

> **黑天鹅：**砂金先生，你知道自己在说些什么吗？

**Aventurine:** Look, I didn't believe it at first either. But the Nameless, as it happens, had quite a unique background that led her to devise a brilliant solution.

> **砂金：**瞧，一开始我也不相信。但这位无名客，恰好有一个特殊的出身，让她想到了个绝妙的手段。

**Aventurine:** Using the technology from the Zorina-Urd system, she gave her life to the Memory Zone, giving Penacony's Dreamscape a "unique signature."

> **砂金：**借助卓瑞娜-乌尔德星系的技术，她将生命献给忆域，赋予了匹诺康尼的梦境一个「独有特征」。

**Aventurine:** When Dreamchasers wander into the Memory Zone, they'll drift towards places that can fulfill their "desires."

> **砂金：**每当有逐梦客误入忆域，他们会因此漂往能实现他们「渴求」的地方。

**Aventurine:** Those who yearn for freedom will witness the form of the Primordial Dreamscape, those consumed by obsession might encounter certain special dream bubbles.

> **砂金：**向往自由之人，将会见到原始梦境的样貌；执念难消之人，或许会接触到某些特殊的梦泡。

**Aventurine:** And those who are "lost and directionless" will return to the Twelve Hours, seeking their path within their own past.

> **砂金：**而「满心茫然，不知去处」的人，将会重返十二时刻，于自身的过往中，寻回方向。

**Aventurine:** The Family discovered this trait and cleverly utilized it, allowing guests to be sent to different Hours upon their first dream entry.

> **砂金：**家族发现了这一特性，巧加利用，让客人初次入梦时，能被送入不同的时刻。

**Aventurine:** In other words, this Nameless is the true founder of the Planet of Festivities.

> **砂金：**换句话说，这位无名客，是盛会之星真正的奠基人。

**Aventurine:** As for me... Ha, maybe I ran into a "good doctor." From the look of this place, he's the one who let me blunder my way in.

> **砂金：**至于我——哈，也许是碰上了个「好医生」。看这附近的风景，是他让我误打误撞闯了进来。

"The impossible in dreams is not 'Death,' but 'Dormancy.' Do stay alive. I wish you the best of luck."

**Aventurine:** Looks like I'm pretty lucky! I can't wait to see what I'll discover here.

> **砂金：**看来我相当好运，能在这里发现什么，我很期待。

**The Dahlia:** It would seem luck favors you still, Mr. Aventurine.

> **大丽花：**还是你的运气更好，砂金先生。

**The Dahlia:** Fate is truly... fascinating yet cruel.

> **大丽花：**命运真是…迷人而又残忍啊。

**The Dahlia:** Remember Miss Robin's question? If the Dreammaster was after the Propagation, why did he have to harm the Glamoth Knight?

> **大丽花：**还记得知更鸟小姐的疑问吗？若是「梦主」意在「繁育」，何必非得加害格拉默铁骑？

**The Dahlia:** Years ago, he had already completed this plan, waiting for a Glamoth Knight to enter the dream. Unfortunately, with the sudden fall of the empire, his hopes seemed to have been dashed.

> **大丽花：**多年之前，他已经完成了这个计划，只等一位格拉默铁骑闯入梦中。可惜，帝国突然覆灭，他的希望似已落空。

**The Dahlia:** Yet just as the Charmony Festival was about to begin, a descendant of the Glamoth Knights appeared uninvited, and everything began to turn once again.

> **大丽花：**偏偏在谐乐大典即将开幕的时刻，一位格拉默铁骑的末裔不请自来，一切再度开始转动。

**The Dahlia:** The Dreammaster was never afraid of Firefly investigating the Propagation. That was exactly the truth he wanted her to see.

> **大丽花：**「梦主」从不害怕流萤深入调查「繁育」，那正是「梦主」希望她看到的真相。

**Black Swan:** Wait, is that why you trapped her in the Memory Zone?

> **黑天鹅：**等等，你是因此才将她困入忆域？

**The Dahlia:** Correct. This place... is where the Dreammaster wanted her to be. And he wanted her to think she'd figured something out, only to make the wrong decision at the most critical moment.

> **大丽花：**没错，这里…本就是「梦主」希望她来到的地方。并且，希望她自以为掌握了些什么，在最关键的时刻，作出错误的判断。

**Black Swan:** So what exactly is the Dreammaster plotting?

> **黑天鹅：**所以，梦主究竟在谋划些什么？

**The Dahlia:** It doesn't matter anymore. We've missed our chance to stop him.

> **大丽花：**已经不重要了，我们错过了阻止他的机会。

**Aventurine:** ...Even if we catch him in person?

> **砂金：**…哪怕揪出他本人？

**The Dahlia:** From the very beginning, he never existed "anywhere" at all.

> **大丽花：**从一开始，他就不存在于「任何地方」。

**The Dahlia:** When he attempted to merge with the Dreamscape, he transformed himself into four Primordial Divine Laws.

> **大丽花：**自他尝试融入梦境时，他就将自身化为四条「太初律令」。

**Echo of "Divine Law (I)":** "11:15█The gods bestowed upon us heavenly fire, and with it divine tribulations. Let them be, for we are all mortals, born to love and to suffer."

> **「律令·其一」的回声：**「11：15█神明赐予我们天火，也赐予我们神圣的磨难。便令它们存在吧，你我皆属凡人，生来便要去爱，去受痛苦。」

*(Floating words)*

Path

Gift

**The Dahlia:** The First Law... As his parting gift, he bestowed it upon Robin, guiding her onto the Harmony.

> **大丽花：**第一条律令，他作为临别赠礼，施予知更鸟，点拨她走上「同谐」。

**Echo of "Divine Law (II)":** "11:30█ Repent and return to righteousness. Though you soar like an eagle and make your nest among the stars, I will bring you down from there."

> **「律令·其二」的回声：**「11：30█改邪归正。你虽如大鹰高飞，在星宿之间搭窝，但我必从那里拉下你来。」

*(Floating words)*

Oppression

Restriction

**The Dahlia:** The Second Law is the power he bestowed upon me as his collaborator. At the same time, it serves as a constraint to ensure I won't act recklessly.

> **大丽花：**第二条律令，是他分予我这个合作者的力量。同时，也作为禁制，确保我不会轻举妄动。

**The Dahlia:** And the Third Law...

> **大丽花：**而第三条律令……

**Echo of "Divine Law (III)":** "11:45█Paradise shall fall into this world of sorrow, and once dawn breaks, it will shatter in the light of day. How could anything golden abide in glory forever?"

> **「律令·其三」的回声：**「11：45█乐园终将跌入愁苦人世，黎明一旦升起，便要坠毁于白昼。凡是金的，怎可能光华长留？」

*(Floating words)*

Obliteration

Paradise

**The Dahlia:** I haven't seen it myself either. According to him, it was an all-out attack, like a torrent of fire pouring down from above.

> **大丽花：**我也还未见识过。据他所说，是倾其所有，悬河注火般的一击。

**Black Swan:** And the last one?

> **黑天鹅：**最后一条呢？

**The Dahlia:** Nobody knows. After claiming to be ill and withdrawing from public view, even though the constraint remained in place, he never contacted anyone again. Not even me, his collaborator.

**The Dahlia:** It's just my theory, but I'm pretty sure I'm right. By then he had finally completed the last "law" and used it to eliminate the one weakness that exists in any plan... the mastermind himself.

**The Dahlia:** The moment the plan was set in motion, Gopher Wood died a martyr's death for the just cause he believed in.

**The Dahlia:** And the Swarm Disaster shall reappear again in the sweet dream.

Returning to Trailblazer's POV...

When you have a chance to make a choice, make one that you know you won't regret.

**The Dahlia:** The moment has finally come. Resentful from being deceived, confused by conflicting memories... Your expression right now is so similar to what I had imagined.

- ▸ Whose side are you really on?

**The Dahlia:** Every side. I'll try to fulfill Duke Inferno's last wishes, advance Gopher Wood's plans, and yes, indeed, I've helped the Stellaron Hunters uncover the truth.

**The Dahlia:** Don't be too mad. I wasn't a Stellaron Hunter back then, but wasn't everything that happened in Penacony the perfect opportunity to join?

- ▸ What did you do to Firefly?

**The Dahlia:** Though it wasn't my original intention, I wanted to help her, to keep her from getting caught up in Gopher Wood's schemes and facing such a detestable fate.

**The Dahlia:** Alas, I couldn't make it happen. The path your predecessor Razalina left in the memoria pushed Firefly towards a new lease on life.

**The Dahlia:** Indeed, how could a Nameless who walks the path of the Trailblaze leave behind a legacy built on "evading destiny"?

- ▸ What's the deal with the Dreammaster?

**The Dahlia:** As you can see, he became a martyr, leaving all his power in the Twelve Hours, like a self-turning hand of time, driving his plans forward.

**The Dahlia:** The Order truly is a harsh Path. Ena, Gopher Wood, and Sunday, the one you know even better... They all chose to sacrifice themselves for it.

**The Dahlia:** But then, something interesting happened: You turned out to be that "variable."

**The Dahlia:** As Aventurine said, Razalina's legacy would send dreamers to where they truly yearned to be. For Firefly, that place turned out to be by your side.

**The Dahlia:** She revealed her true self to you, reuniting with you as a Stellaron Hunter.

**The Dahlia:** Then, guided by a History Fictionologist, you witnessed the life of the Watchmaker Mikhail.

**The Dahlia:** It was truly a life worth remembering, worth giving everything for. I would've loved to raise a glass of "Hello and Goodbye" to him, just like the one who saw things through to the end, Mr. Gallagher. What a shame I can't do it properly here.

**The Dahlia:** Sunday also came here, feigning cooperation, to reveal to you the truth about Penacony. The foundation of the sweet dream, hidden behind the Grand Theater's curtain, was actually a Stellaron, and the true mastermind behind everything was none other than the Dreammaster Gopher Wood.

**The Dahlia:** But you know now that his words weren't entirely true. What followed was one of the few beautiful memories that Firefly has held onto to this day...

**The Dahlia:** In order to win tickets to the Grand Theater, you and Firefly participated in the Moment of Scorchsand auditions. Your coordination was so perfect, like you were companions who'd long shared the same rhythm.

**The Dahlia:** See? Even though you've forgotten everything from before, some traces remain etched in this moment, becoming part of your instincts.

**The Dahlia:** Until... following the "script," Firefly sent you all to the final stage before departing on her own. In those beautiful moments before that, she experienced the joy of living, yet she still had to... face the terror of death.

> **大丽花：**没人知道。在他自称抱恙在身，不再现身后，尽管禁制仍未消去，但连我这位合作者，他也没有再联络过。

**The Dahlia:** However, you may have noticed that during this period there was a small gap. She and you weren't together the whole time.

> **大丽花：**这只是我的猜测，但多半属实——那时他终于完成了最后的「律令」，并借此，抹除了任何计划都会存在的破绽…谋划者本人，他自己。

**The Dahlia:** You returned to a child's dream through a dream bubble left by Mikhail and witnessed his legacy. However, Firefly was not a Trailblazer, so she couldn't enter it.

> **大丽花：**在计划开始转动的瞬间，歌斐木便已为了他信奉的公义，殉道而死。

**The Dahlia:** While waiting alone, she met her true collaborator... and thus learned that there was more than one legacy in Penacony, and more than one Watchmaker.

> **大丽花：**而「寰宇蝗灾」，也将重现于美梦之中。

Firefly's memories are slowly emerging...

Back to Firefly's POV, she was entering the final stage of the Dreammaster's plot in an unexpected turn of events...

**Argenti:** In the name of Beauty, the One and Only has brought me to this place. Perhaps it is destiny's guidance that led me here. In this dreamscape tainted by the Propagation, I shall uphold the name of Beauty and clear the path for all of you!

> **银枝：**「纯美」在上——「希世难得号」竟将我带来了此地。或许正是命运的指引，在这片被「繁育」污浊的梦境中，我将贯彻纯美之名，为诸位荡清前路！

**Aventurine:** ...Ah, passing knight, you've found just the right person. I am an IPC envoy trapped here. Lend me your aid, for Idrila the Beauty is the most peerless beauty of them all!

> **砂金：**…这位路过的骑士，你算是找对人了。我是正被困于此地的公司使节——请您伸出援手吧，纯美女神伊德莉拉美貌盖世无双！

**Black Swan:** Someone's a quick learner...

> **黑天鹅：**学得倒快……

> **黑天鹅：**不准备说点什么了？这巧合有些荒诞了。

> **大丽花：**还能说什么呢？难道这位骑士先生，会听我们慢慢讲道理？

**The Dahlia:** That's the fascinating thing about destiny. Some people are just born lucky. Let them be. We should get moving now.

> **大丽花：**这也是命运的迷人之处，有些人天生就是如此好运，由他们去吧，我们应当快点动身了。

> **黑天鹅：**…要去哪儿？

**The Dahlia:** To find your dance partner, darling. By an unexpected turn of events, the Dreammaster's plan is about to come to fruition, and to deal with him...

> **大丽花：**去找你的那位舞伴，亲爱的。阴差阳错，梦主的计划将要实现，要想对付他……

**The Dahlia:** ...We'll need an Emanator to step in.

> **大丽花：**…非得有一位令使出手不可。

Not long after, in the Moment of Scorchsand, at the end of the sermon...

**Sunday:** Since the future of the Stellaron, Penacony, and even the entire Cosmos is at stake, let's draw a conclusion there, in all fairness.

> **星期日：**事关星核、匹诺康尼、乃至整个银河的未来。公平起见，就让我们在那里一见真章。

**March 7th:** Does that mean he wants to fight us during the Charmony Festival?

> **三月七：**他的意思，是要我们去谐乐大典上一较高下吗？

**Firefly:** I'm afraid so.

> **流萤：**恐怕就是这个意思。

**Himeko:** Anyway, we can't leave the Stellaron unchecked. This is about Trailblazing a bright future for Penacony and fulfilling the long-cherished wishes of Mikhail and our other predecessors.

> **姬子：**无论如何，星穹列车不能对星核坐视不理。为匹诺康尼「开拓」未来，也是米哈伊尔等前人的夙愿。

**Firefly:** ...

**Firefly:** (So that's how it is. Well then, it's time for me to depart.)

> **流萤：**（原来是这样啊，那么，我也该出发了。）

> **流萤：**利用一整个世界的意志，推动一位星神的降生…这场对决绝不是单纯力量的交锋。为了匹诺康尼的未来，不能只有你们在舞台上战斗。

**March 7th:** What do you mean? Are you not coming with us...?

> **三月七：**你们？什么意思，你不跟我们一起走了吗……

**Himeko:** I believe Firefly is trying to say that she's heading off to another battle.

> **姬子：**我想，流萤小姐的意思是，她要赶赴另一片战场了。

**Firefly:** Mhmm.

> **流萤：**嗯。

**Firefly:** ...Before I left, Destiny's Slave told me that this journey would bring unforgettable rewards. Even though the script he gave me only had a few lines, I couldn't ignore them.

> **流萤：**…出发前，「命运的奴隶」告诉我，此行会让我得到难以忘怀的收获。他给出的剧本只有寥寥数行，却让人难以忽视。

**Firefly:** Because one of the lines said... I'll experience "death" three times in the land of the dreams.

> **流萤：**因为其中一行写着…我会在梦想之地经历三次「死亡」。

**March 7th:** Three times!? This can't be serious, right...?

> **三月七：**三、三次死亡？！这一定是打引号的吧……

**Firefly:** The first time was a painful death when I was stabbed by the blade of "Dormancy," which led to all subsequent events. The script will always come true, but in a way... that will only be revealed when that page is turned.

> **流萤：**第一次是如同死亡般的痛苦，我的身体被「沉眠」的翼刃贯穿，才有了后来所有的故事。剧本必定会应验，但形式…只在翻开那一页时才会显露。

**Firefly:** But the death granted to me by the Planet of Festivities was merciful enough.

> **流萤：**但盛会之星给予我的死亡，已经足够仁慈。

**Firefly:** For my first death, I chose how it would come and what it would change. For the next two, I want to try my best to achieve the same outcome.

> **流萤：**第一次死亡，我曾自己决定了它如何到来，又将改变什么。接下来的两次，我也想尽力得到相同的结果。

**Himeko:** Have you... made up your mind, Firefly?

> **姬子：**流萤小姐，你…已经做好觉悟了吗？

**Himeko:** Thank you again for your assistance for the Astral Express. May we meet again in reality.

> **姬子：**再次感谢你对星穹列车提供的帮助。祝愿我们在现实中再见。

**Firefly:** Yeah, farewell, everyone. May your Trailblazing expedition never end.

> **流萤：**嗯，再见，各位。愿你们的「开拓」之旅——永不终结。

> **流萤：**一株破土而生的新蕊，

> **流萤：**它迎着朝阳绽放，

**Firefly:** Like fireflies to a flame... life begets death.

Oak Dream — The Place of Birth, The Place of Death

**Firefly:** Is this the place...

> **流萤：**…「梦主」？！

**Firefly:** ...Where the Dreammaster executes all his schemes?

**"Dreammaster" Gopher Wood (?):** Or perhaps, it's the place where he'll find redemption.

**Firefly:** ...Dreammaster!?

**"Dreammaster" Gopher Wood (?):** No... just one of his "divine laws".

**Firefly:** Don't think I'll let my guard down. I know your true nature. Whatever your goal is, I will stop you.

**"Divine Law (III)":** No need to be so tense, Miss Firefly. I am but a thought formed from memory. Feel free to strike me if you wish to verify this.

> **「律令•其三」：**不必如此紧张，流萤小姐。我不过是一段记忆凝成的思绪。你大可出手攻击，确认此事。

**"Divine Law (III)":** But his actions aren't baseless. There's a reason for my presence here.

> **「律令•其三」：**「梦主」将行的恶毒之举，我同样一无所知，但他一直在等待你的到来。那位他亲手杀死的格拉默铁骑，他希望以我的存在，还她一个「公道」，并由你，她的友人来见证。

> **流萤：**…公道？

> **「律令•其三」：**不错，我对此处还算熟悉，足以为您引路。对你来说，这也有其意义——见证他的忏悔，也就等同于，揭晓他的罪行。

> **流萤：**我不需要你来引路，也能做到这件事。

**"Divine Law (III)":** But you need someone to decipher the truth. Force alone won't solve anything.

### Uncover the truth

> *The Dream of Gnawing Oak, the place where the Dreammaster executes all his schemes... Of course, the words of the laws can't be taken at face value. Since I've ended up here by a twist of fate, I'll see if I can find more clues about the Dreammaster.*

**Firefly:** What is this place?

> **流萤：**这是什么地方？

**"Divine Law (III)":** Oak Manor, once part of the Dewlight Pavilion. Before becoming the Dreammaster, he used to hold family councils here, but it's been abandoned for years now, sinking into the Primordial Memory Zone.

> **「律令•其三」：**橡木公馆，曾是朝露公馆的一部分。成为「梦主」前，他常常在此举行家系议会，但如今已废弃多年，沉入了原始忆域。

*(Activate Crank to create a path)*

**Firefly:** Is that...

> **流萤：**那是……

**"Divine Law (III)":** I'm afraid it's... pain.

> **「律令•其三」：**恐怕，是苦痛。

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Firefly:** Did Oak Manor always have decor like this?

> **流萤：**橡木公馆原本就有这种装饰吗？

**"Divine Law (III)":** After taking over the sweet dream, he never turned away any sincere dream seekers, even if they were shady characters.

> **「律令•其三」：**入主美梦后，他从不拒绝任何有心逐梦之人，即便对方是狡诈恶徒。

**"Divine Law (III)":** Walking along the Harmony, he firmly believed that even villains could be transformed by Xipe's benevolence. To show mercy, act with justice, and walk with humility.

> **「律令•其三」：**行于「同谐」，他深信恶徒也能被希佩的仁爱感化。好怜悯，行公义，谦卑行走。

**Firefly:** It seems his wishes didn't come true.

> **流萤：**他似乎没能如愿。

**"Divine Law (III)":** Indeed, those villains never learned their lesson. They even conspired with one another, foolishly attempting to establish their own family lineage. Such arrogance! They went as far as sending a messenger across the vast cosmos to seek permission from the Main Family.

> **「律令•其三」：**不错，恶徒从未醒悟，甚至彼此勾结，妄图成立一个家系。他们是何等狂妄，为此，竟请一位信使跨越迢迢寰宇，请求「主家」的许可。

**"Divine Law (III)":** In the end, they only brought shame upon themselves.

> **「律令•其三」：**结果，他们只能自取其辱。

**Firefly:** And then there was a revolt?

> **流萤：**然后就发生了暴乱？

**"Divine Law (III)":** Fortunately, his heart was already aligned with the Oak Family, allowing him to survive by sheer luck... though he could never leave the sweet dream again.

> **「律令•其三」：**好在他与橡木家系早已异体同心，侥幸生还——却再也无法脱离美梦。

**"Divine Law (III)":** Soon after, the manor was decorated this way.

> **「律令•其三」：**不久后，公馆便有了这番布置。

**Firefly:** Was he trying to warn himself?

> **流萤：**他想用来警醒自己吗？

**"Who is Imprisoned in Dreams":** If your beliefs lead you down a path of plight, why stubbornly cling to them?

> **「何人囚于梦中」：**若你信奉之事，将你带到这般田地，何必执迷不改？

**"Divine Law (III)":** ...That's exactly what I'm getting at.

> **「律令•其三」：**——正是提醒此事。

**Firefly:** At least now... I can understand those feelings.

> **流萤：**至少现在…我能够理解这种心情。

**"Divine Law (III)":** And I think I've come to understand why I exist here.

> **「律令•其三」：**而我似乎也已理解，自己为何会存在于此。

**"Divine Law (III)":** At that moment, Gopher Wood stood at a crossroads, pondering whether the Harmony was still the right path. But my fragmented memories have not yet revealed where this would ultimately lead...

> **「律令•其三」：**那时，歌斐木正立于岔路，开始思索，「同谐」是否仍为正道。最终行向何处，我残缺的记忆并未点明……

**"Divine Law (III)":** Since he was about to commit a great evil, perhaps the Dreammaster who left behind these laws was hoping I would choose a different path.

> **「律令•其三」：**但既然他将要行下大恶，留下律令的「梦主」，恐怕是在期待我走向另一种可能。

**Firefly:** But it's too late to change anything now.

> **流萤：**可这来不及改变任何事了。

**"Divine Law (III)":** Not necessarily. We might as well keep moving forward. Good intentions are always the final creation of evil deeds, for they know they must be stopped. And his past choices will reveal his schemes to us.

> **「律令•其三」：**未必，我们不妨继续动身。善念总是恶行的最后造物，因它知晓自身应被制止。而他曾经的选择，也将为我们揭示他的阴谋。

"Family's Envoy, Awoken Mode": But you survived by sheer luck, and I set out for this very reason. You must settle this matter yourself and reclaim your lost respect... not for yourself, but for the Family.

**Dreammaster Gopher Wood:** ...To survive, but without the power to do anything.

"Family's Envoy, Awoken Mode": I shall bestow upon you another crime. Its evil reaches even the heavens, and once known, the name Gopher Wood will lose all meaning. You will be alive in body but dead in spirit.

"Family's Envoy, Awoken Mode": People believe it to be a creation of the Destruction, for it always arrives coupled with calamity.

"Family's Envoy, Awoken Mode": I shall clear the shadows before your eyes, so that you may once again behold that almighty force that giveth and taketh, and worship the mercy bestowed by the Triple Face...

> **「梦主」歌斐木：**——在我看来，你能够怀抱希望，正因未曾直面注定的死亡。

**Dreammaster Gopher Wood:** Would you blame me? Would you, Mikhail?

> **「梦主」歌斐木：**你会怪罪我么？米哈伊尔，你会么？

**Dreammaster Gopher Wood:** I tried to unveil Xipe's third face, but I was wrong. It wasn't the Propagation. Yet destiny was so merciful, making me realize...

> **「梦主」歌斐木：**我试着揭开希佩的第三重面相，可我错了，那并非「繁育」。可命运如此仁慈，借此令我发觉——

**Dreammaster Gopher Wood:** The Propagation was able to continuously evoke people's prayers for the Order through the fear brought by the Swarm. When the Swarm Disaster ravaged the universe, that was also when the Order reached its unprecedented peak.

> **「梦主」歌斐木：**「繁育」竟能以蝗灾造就的恐惧，不断唤起人们对「秩序」的祈求。寰宇蝗灾肆虐之时，亦是「秩序」空前鼎盛之时。

**Dreammaster Gopher Wood:** But after that, the Great One deliberately ignited a war among gods, leading to THEIR own downfall. THEY would rather fall than let the Order ascend upon a staircase of falsehoods.

> **「梦主」歌斐木：**可此后，神主却主动掀起神战，促成了自身的陨落——祂不希望「秩序」，踩上谎言的神阶。

**Dreammaster Gopher Wood:** THEY were wrong too.

> **「梦主」歌斐木：**祂也错了。

**Dreammaster Gopher Wood:** The Great One wished to restore justice to the mortal world, but what people prayed for wasn't justice. It was "freedom from fear."

> **「梦主」歌斐木：**神主欲将公义还于人世，可人们向他祈求的并非公义，而是「无需恐惧」。

**Dreammaster Gopher Wood:** Will you forgive me? Will you, Mikhail?

> **「梦主」歌斐木：**你会赦免我么？米哈伊尔，你会么？

**Dreammaster Gopher Wood:** I shall commit an act of transgression, mending the errors of the gods. I will restore the missing hand to the celestial clock and show mankind death.

> **「梦主」歌斐木：**我将行僭越之事，修缮神明的谬误——我会将缺失的指针置回众星的大钟，示人以死。

**Dreammaster Gopher Wood:** I know my sins. They are ever before me. But I shall never repent.

> **「梦主」歌斐木：**我知我罪，我罪常在我前——但我绝不悔改。

**Firefly:** Using the Propagation to lay the foundation for an eternal paradise... So what he meant was...

> **流萤：**用「繁育」为永恒乐园奠基…那时候他的意思是……

**Firefly:** Recreate the Swarm Disaster, using fear to force people to... pray for the Order's return?

> **流萤：**重现寰宇蝗灾，用恐惧迫使人们…祈求「秩序」再次降临？

**"Divine Law (III)":** Perhaps that's why your friend was killed. The Dreammaster wanted to find out how the Glamoth Iron Cavalry obtained the Propagation's power.

> **「律令•其三」：**或许您的朋友正是因此遇害——「梦主」想要借此知晓，格拉默铁骑是如何得到「繁育」的力量。

**Firefly:** That's not a power that can be easily controlled. We need to pick up the pace.

> **流萤：**那不是能被轻易掌控的力量，我们得加快脚步了。

### Continue to investigate the Dreammaster's schemes

> *The Dreammaster's intentions are now painfully clear... I have to stop him. But how on earth does he plan to pull all this off?*

*(Floating texts)*

Paradise have I wrought,

Yet men discern it not.

In the fairest place,

For they deem

None worthy of felicity.

the Order will surely return

> **「律令•其三」：**通过此处，便是橡木公馆尽头的大厅。

**"Divine Law (III)":** And before us lies his final decision: where to go after turning away from the Harmony.

> **「律令•其三」：**而我们面前的，是他最后的抉择——背离「同谐」后，走向何方。

**Firefly:** Since he wants you to choose again, which side will you take?

> **流萤：**既然他希望你再选一次，你想走哪一边？

**"Divine Law (III)":** Go ahead with whichever path you think is right. I'll take the other one.

> **「律令•其三」：**就按你的判断前行吧。而我会步入余下的那条。

**Firefly:** ...?

> **流萤：**…？

**"Divine Law (III)":** Don't worry, I can't change anything. Even if I have a sudden awakening, it would only give the Dreammaster peace of mind.

> **「律令•其三」：**放心，我改变不了任何事。即便我幡然醒悟，也只是让梦主求得心安。

**"Divine Law (III)":** He deserves absolute punishment, and must be brought to heel.

> **「律令•其三」：**他更应当遭受绝罚，受人制止。

**Firefly:** So you made a different choice after all.

> **流萤：**那么你还是做出了不同的选择。

**Firefly:** I will stop him.

> **流萤：**我会阻止他的。

**"Divine Law (III)":** I hope everything goes well. This is goodbye then.

> **「律令•其三」：**但愿一切顺利——我们就此告别吧。
