**Firefly:** No doubt about it, this is the place.

> **流萤：**不会有错，就是这里。

**"Divine Law (III)":** My apologies for keeping you waiting, Lady Firefly.

> **「律令•其三」：**有失远迎，流萤女士。

**Firefly:** Ah, it's you. It seems the other path leads here too.

> **流萤：**是你，另一条路也通往此处。

**Firefly:** ...

> **流萤：**……

**Firefly:** What is this?

> **流萤：**这是什么？

**"Divine Law (III)":** It's the terror that humanity dares not recall: the Swarm Disaster's Chrysalis of Extinction.

> **「律令•其三」：**是世人不愿回想的恐惧，寰宇蝗灾的「死灭之蛹」。

**"Divine Law (III)":** The Dreammaster's devastating strike, like a torrent of fire, lies hidden behind the stained glass, waiting to shatter it and unleash the Swarm Disaster once again.

> **「律令•其三」：**「梦主」悬河注火般的一击，也正藏于彩窗之后，只待将其戳破，再度催生寰宇蝗灾。

**Firefly:** ...How did the Dreammaster become this powerful?

> **流萤：**…「梦主」怎么会强大到这种程度？

**Firefly:** We need to stop him.

> **流萤：**我们得阻止他。

**"Divine Law (III)":** Or maybe... stop me.

> **「律令•其三」：**或许，是阻止我。

**Firefly:** (As expected...)

> **流萤：**（果然……）

**"Divine Law (III)":** I shall end this matter on his behalf. This thought, together with the power beyond the window, completes a single, true "divine law."

> **「律令•其三」：**我将代他了结此事——这道思绪，连同窗外的力量，才是完整的一句「律令」。

**Firefly:** ...Divine law?

> **流萤：**…律令？

**"Divine Law (III)":** Everything you saw along the way, none of that was meant for you. It was meant for me, so that I could bring you justice.

> **「律令•其三」：**途中所见的一切，不是为你准备的，而是为我——为了还你公道。

**Firefly:** ...I don't see any justice in any of this.

> **流萤：**…我看不出哪里公道。

**"Divine Law (III)":** Lady Firefly, I assume you've pondered over destiny too?

> **「律令•其三」：**流萤女士，想必您也思索过命运？

**Firefly:** I don't think it can be used as an excuse for anything.

> **流萤：**我不认为它能够成为任何事的借口。

**Firefly:** Even if the ending is predetermined, we can still decide how we get there.

> **流萤：**即使结局已经注定，但过程，我们还是可以自己决定。

**"Divine Law (III)":** That's just self-deception. You've never been able to change the course of events.

> **「律令•其三」：**这不过是「自我欺骗」——就连过程，你也从未改变过。

**"Divine Law (III)":** When we stand at the crossroads of choice, no matter how many times we start over, we'll always make the same decision. By that moment in time, we've already been shaped by our past, believing that choice to be the only correct path.

> **「律令•其三」：**站在抉择前的你我，无论重来多少次，也只会作出同一个决定——彼时彼刻，我们早已被过往塑形，认为那才是唯一正确的道路。

**"Divine Law (III)":** All the past you've left behind has already determined your future.

> **「律令•其三」：**所有你抛在身后的过去，早已决定了你的未来。

**"Divine Law (III)":** Your friend tried to escape destiny, only to meet the same end. And now, here you are, no different.

> **「律令•其三」：**你的朋友，她试着逃离命运，最终只得到相同的结局。此时此刻，你也亦然。

**Firefly:** ...

> **流萤：**……

**Firefly:** But the person you met today is someone who has rewritten their destiny.

> **流萤：**但你今天见到的，恰恰是改写过命运的人。

**"Divine Law (III)":** Well then, I'll offer you my sympathy, Hunter. As far as I know, there's only one way to rewrite destiny.

> **「律令•其三」：**那么，我愿意奉上同情，猎手——就我所知，改写命运的方式只有一种。

**"Divine Law (III)":** Give up your true desires and stop walking your own path. In doing so, your destiny will indeed change, and you may even sway the course of the world.

> **「律令•其三」：**放弃自己真正的渴求，不再按照自己的意愿前行。如此，你的命运确会不同，甚至能够左右世界的去向。

**"Divine Law (III)":** But from that moment on, you will have killed yourself... alive in body, but dead in spirit.

> **「律令•其三」：**但从那一刻起，你便杀死了自己，虽生犹死。

**Firefly:** This is the price we're willing to pay to defy destiny. Not everyone chooses to surrender with their hands up.

> **流萤：**为了反抗命运，这是我们愿意承受的代价——不是所有人都会选择举手投降。

**Firefly:** "Everyone has the power to see the world as it is, and to bear that weight. This is the true meaning of sacrifice."

> **流萤：**「任何人都有能力认清世界，然后承担它的重量，这才是牺牲的意义。」

**"Divine Law (III)":** I admire your resolve. Since you don't mind sacrifice...

> **「律令•其三」：**我敬佩你的觉悟，既然你不介意牺牲——

**"Divine Law (III)":** That's enough talk. Stand down, Hunter. You can't stop that strike.

> **「律令•其三」：**辩言到此为止，退去吧，猎手，你无法阻挡那一击。

**Firefly:** I won't let you get away with this.

> **流萤：**我不会让你得逞。

**"Divine Law (III)":** Oh really? How did the Harmony succeed in spreading its cancer across the universe then?

> **「律令•其三」：**是么？「同谐」使恶癌遍布寰宇，为何便能得逞？

**"Divine Law (III)":** ...?

> **「律令•其三」：**…？

**Firefly:** Though she wasn't my true companion, that Cremator left me with a spark of her flame.

> **流萤：**虽然她不是我真正的同伴，但那位焚化工，为我留下过一簇她的火焰。

**Firefly:** I never trusted you. During our journey together, I planted it on you, and now, I'll let it reignite. It can burn away a thread of thought with ease.

> **流萤：**我从未信任过你，同行途中，我把它留在了你的身上——现在，我让它重新燃起，烧尽一道思绪轻而易举。

**"Divine Law (III)":** Impressive, but do you really think you can get ahead of me?

> **「律令•其三」：**令人赞叹，但你当真认为，自己能快我一步？

**Firefly:** If I can't, I still won't run away. If that strike is inevitable, I'll be the only one who dies in this nightmare.

> **流萤：**如果不能，我也不会逃开。如果那一击必然到来，在这场噩梦里死去的，只会是我一个人。

**"Divine Law (III)":** Your courage shines like pure gold. Even so...

> **「律令•其三」：**您的勇气如真金般辉耀，然而——

**"Divine Law (III)":** "11:45█Paradise shall fall into this world of sorrow, and once dawn breaks, it will shatter in the light of day. How could anything golden abide in glory forever?"

> **「律令•其三」：**「11：45█乐园终将跌入愁苦人世，黎明一旦升起，便要坠毁于白昼。凡是金的，怎可能光华长留？」

**"Divine Law (III)":** Come forth and present yourself, last heir of the Empire. I permit you to unveil the true face of Order...

> **「律令•其三」：**那便上前觐见吧，帝国的末裔。我准许你，为我揭示秩序的真容——

**"Divine Law (III)":** I permit you, with mortal hands, to bring forth the Great One!

> **「律令•其三」：**我准许你，以人之手，为我创生神主！

*(Cutscene begins)*

**"Divine Law (III)":** The Swarm will again devour all things...

> **「律令•其三」：**虫鸣将再度啃食万物……

**"Divine Law (III)":** And fill the hearts of the lost with terror.

> **「律令•其三」：**以恐惧充盈失路者的心房。

**"Divine Law (III)":** ...Someone must cast down rage in THEIR stead.

> **「律令•其三」：**——必先有人代祂降下烈怒，

**"Divine Law (III)":** All that lives...

> **「律令•其三」：**举凡活物，

**"Divine Law (III)":** Will one day die.

> **「律令•其三」：**无一不死。

**Firefly:** Even so, I will still do as I've always done,

> **流萤：**即便如此，我还是会一如既往，

**Firefly:** And pray that I can stay alive.

> **流萤：**祈祷自己能够活下去。

**Firefly:** I can do it, right?

> **流萤：**我做得到，对吧？

**"Divine Law (III)":** But "death" will come because you, out of all people...

> **「律令•其三」：**可偏偏那「死地」的降临，正因你在众人当中…

**"Divine Law (III)":** Are the foremost in seeking "life."

> **「律令•其三」：**最先求取「生路」。

*(Cutscene ends)*

Not long ago, 3 system hours until Charmony Festival — Penacony Grand Theater

**Sunday:** Your last confession took me by surprise.

> **星期日：**您的最后一次忏悔，令我深感意外。

**Sunday:** Mr. Gopher Wood, no... the Final Divine Law.

> **星期日：**歌斐木先生，不…「最后的律令」。

**Sunday:** I suspect there was never any Chrysalis of Extinction from the Swarm Disaster to begin with, was there?

> **星期日：**自始至终，恐怕从未有过寰宇蝗灾的「死灭之蛹」吧？

**Sunday:** What exists in Oak Manor is nothing but a disguise. The only thing hidden within is a Stellaron.

> **星期日：**存在于橡木公馆的不过是伪装——藏于其中的，只有星核。

**"Divine Law (IV): Midnight":** True, I never unraveled the secrets of the Propagation. Now split into four parts, a single strike of mine cannot harm that Hunter.

> **「律令•其四•子夜」：**不错，我从未堪破「繁育」之秘，如今一分为四，一击之力，也无法奈何那猎手。

**Sunday:** But if a pathstrider of the same origin as the Swarm were to be deceived by tuning, mistaking what lies before them as certain death... If they were to make a wish "to live" before the Stellaron... The outcome would twist into a catastrophe that brings death: the Swarm Disaster.

> **星期日：**可一位与虫群同源的行者，若是被调律骗过，误以为眼前确为死地，在星核面前许下「想要活着」的愿望，那结果，便将扭曲成致人以死的灾祸，寰宇蝗灾。

**Sunday:** Her life shall "beget death," and that death shall "beget life" unto this world. But why are you so certain that nothing will go wrong?

> **星期日：**以她的「向生而死」，予此世「向死而生」——但您为何如此确信，此事绝无错失？

**"Divine Law (IV): Midnight":** I've already glimpsed the secrets of the Stellaron, and I have no need for certainty here. Even if she never entered the game, that strike would still fall at the appointed time, causing the Stellaron to shatter.

> **「律令•其四•子夜」：**我早已窥得星核之秘，无需确信任何事。若她从未入局，那一击仍会适时降下，令星核就此爆裂。

**"Divine Law (IV): Midnight":** The dreamers will learn that the power to destroy their own lives comes from the Harmony, but that it is ultimately the worst option.

> **「律令•其四•子夜」：**入梦者都将知晓，杀死自己的力量源于「同谐」——但那终究只是下策。

**Sunday:** ...

> **星期日：**……

**Sunday:** You should know that I won't agree with this.

> **星期日：**您该知道，我不会认同。

**"Divine Law (IV): Midnight":** Too late, child. The hands of time have struck midnight. Look up at the stars. Do you know why they are so cruel?

> **「律令•其四•子夜」：**为时已晚，孩子。指针已然落于子夜，抬起头来，目视群星——知道它们因何而残忍吗？

**"Divine Law (IV): Midnight":** That reason shall be the final "divine law." And with this, we entrust everything into your hands...

> **「律令•其四•子夜」：**其原因，将是最后一道「律令」，以此，我等将一切交于你手——

**"Divine Law (IV): Midnight":** "00:00█Because the stars belong to everyone, they have never belonged to anyone. If you love everyone, then you love no one."

> **「律令•其四•子夜」：**「00：00█因众星属于所有人，也便从未属于任何人。若你爱着所有人，便是不爱任何人。」

**"Divine Law (IV): Midnight":** Well, that's where our conversation ends. Go ahead. The 107,336 souls of the Oak Family have dreamed of this moment too many times...

> **「律令•其四•子夜」：**我们言尽于此。动手吧，橡木家系的十万七千三百三十六道灵魂已梦见这一刻太多次了……

**"Divine Law (IV): Midnight":** The opening is near. Go, my child. Seize the power of the Harmony and reveal your karma.

> **「律令•其四•子夜」：**开幕的时刻近了。去吧，孩子。窃夺「同谐」的权柄，揭晓你的报应。

**Sunday:** I shall walk the path of the Order to its end, as you wish. But I can no longer accept your divine laws. If I become the only star in the sky, even if I belonged to no one, there would still be no cruelty.

> **星期日：**「秩序」的道路，我将如您所愿，行至尽头。但您的律令，我已不能认同。若我成为了天空中唯一的星，即便从不属于任何人，也绝无残忍可言。

**"Divine Law (IV): Midnight":** ...?

> **「律令•其四•子夜」：**…？

**Sunday:** The stars are cruel, only because they have never shared their heat with the masses, burning fiercely only for themselves.

> **星期日：**众星残忍，只因他们从未将热力分予万众，只为自身熊熊燃烧。

**Sunday:** Well, there can't be two suns in the sky. I'll step up and take down the other sun if necessary.

> **星期日：**天无二日。如有必要，我会出手将太阳击落。

**Sunday:** Moreover, how could I allow other stars to shine during the day?

> **星期日：**又岂会容许众星，于白昼之中放光？

**Sunday:** Paradise shall soon be created, not at midnight, but at "noon." I shall initiate the first and only divine law of the Great Septimus...

> **星期日：**乐园终将造就，不在子夜，而在「正午」。神主日最初，也是唯一的律令，由我亲启——

**Sunday:** "12:00█I shall ascend to the heavens, becoming the scorching sun. Bathed in my light, my people shall flourish, while all evil shall be eradicated."

> **星期日：**「12：00█我将飞上高空，变作天上的太阳。万众在我的光芒中热烈生长，而一切罪恶将无所遁形。」

**"Fading Last Words":** Very well, child... do what you must. Perhaps I was never without choice, merely too entangled with the fear of losing, never knowing what was possible if I chose to act.

> **「将要消散的遗言」：**也好，孩子…放手而为吧。或许，我也并非选无可选，而是耽于趋利避害，从未知其不可，亦要为之。

**"Fading Last Words":** I should become a Watchmaker too. The harmony I seek has but one note repeating endlessly, never falling out of tune...

> **「将要消散的遗言」：**我应当也去做个「钟表匠」，我所认定的谐乐，仅有一个音节周而复始，永不失准——

**"Fading Last Words":** Life, too, has its time.

> **「将要消散的遗言」：**生亦有时。

**Firefly:** We did it... He faded first, causing that power to lose control.

> **流萤：**成功了，是他先一步消散，让那道力量失去了控制。

**Firefly:** Is that...

> **流萤：**那是……

Fate closed its premonitions, and arrived in due time.

**Firefly:** How... did you...

> **流萤：**你…怎么…

**"Fading Last Words":** Death comes for us all eventually.

Returning to Trailblazer's POV...

When you have a chance to make a choice, make one that you know you won't regret.

**The Dahlia:** I'm starting to hate how powerless I am. How I wish that I had some way to comfort you right at this moment. Don't worry. That was all in the past.

- ▸ What happened to Firefly?

> ▸ 你到底是哪边的？

**The Dahlia:** She had almost reached her demise, yet she survived. Her second death hadn't come yet. It would be upon her soon.

- ▸ What did the Dreammaster do?

> ▸ 你把流萤怎么了？

**The Dahlia:** He lured a Glamoth Iron Cavalry to face a Stellaron after years of plotting. Though everything Firefly had learned since entering the dream was true, they were only truths that the Dreammaster wanted her to see.

**The Dahlia:** And that... is often regarded as the highest form of deception.

**The Dahlia:** The Stellaron answered Firefly's desire to keep living. In response, it recreated within the sweet dream the Swarm Disaster... and the Aeon Ena.

- ▸ ...The Swarm Disaster?

> ▸ 梦主是怎么回事？

**The Dahlia:** It was confined within the dream. Even a Stellaron could not truly revive the Swarm Disaster. The Dreammaster's actions could only evoke fear in those already dreaming.

**The Dahlia:** Yet this Swarm was frighteningly real when you considered the next steps of his plan... Ena's Dream had enveloped the entire star system, which meant the Swarm Disaster would gradually become indistinguishable from reality.

**The Dahlia:** The Propagation would awaken the most primal fears in all dreamers. Through their cries, the Path of Order would be sustained... until the entire cosmos drowned in a singular dream.

**The Dahlia:** You engaged in a decisive battle with Sunday at the Penacony Grand Theater afterward. He had, without question, become an Emanator at that time after usurping the Harmonious Choir.

**The Dahlia:** But you showed no fear and gave it your all in your fight against him, and you emerged victorious with the help of your allies.

**The Dahlia:** Yet that battle, the setbacks, the victory... was just an illusion woven by Sunday. Ena's Dream had already enveloped the entire Asdana System.

**The Dahlia:** It was impossible for you to beat Sunday at that time. Ena's Dream was so terrifying that even Memokeepers could not act freely in it.

**The Dahlia:** Fortunately, we sought out Acheron first, and she lent us a helping hand. That allowed us to take action along with that quarrelsome "friend" of ours and try waking people one by one.

**The Dahlia:** Indeed. Don't forget that, even within the sweet dream, there were still many people yearning, striving, and pushing forward with all their might.

**The Dahlia:** At that moment, you, too, would face a similar deathmatch. Being woken up by Black Swan was a watershed moment. Due to the existence of Ena's Dream, your memories from that point onward can no longer be viewed from the same perspective as before.

*(Oneric Memoir opens and everything beyond Trailblazer: Confronting Sunday burns away)*

**The Dahlia:** It's not that they're "not real enough." Things always look different when viewed from different angles... Ever thought about whether we've truly seen our authentic selves in a mirror?

**The Dahlia:** And from that moment forth... I would also no longer be able to help you.

**The Dahlia:** Your memories belong to you alone at the end of the day. Destiny only allows me to intervene this far. However, you must remember this...

**The Dahlia:** How your memories manifest is ultimately up to you.

**The Dahlia:** People can't dream of things they've never seen before. They're afraid to wake up because the sweet dream is woven from their own past, a past they still can't let go of.

**The Dahlia:** It's just like your actions at this very moment... Only by embracing the past can you find the strength to walk towards the future.

**The Dahlia:** Firefly, me, the Stellaron Hunters, this entire world... We're all waiting for you to reach that moment.

**The Dahlia:** I can hardly tell if this is nervousness or excitement. (Trailblazer)... You are different, unique, and worth waiting for.

**The Dahlia:** Go, and write a prelude to memories once more!

*(Tap and hold to retrieve memories)*

**The Dahlia:** From within the ashes of time... You must reignite "destiny."

*(Unlock Achievement The Past Is the Future's Reflection)*

## 230　Every Dawn of the World　世间的每一个清晨

> 世界：Penacony　|　区域：The Reverie (Reality)　|　角色：Firefly; Silver Wolf; Acheron; The Dahlia; Trailblazer; Robin; Sunday; Black Swan; Dan Heng; Aventurine; Boothill; Sparkle; Siobhan; Argenti; Gallagher; Misha; March 7th; Welt; Himeko; Dominicus; Ena; Dr. Ratio; Jade; Pom-Pom　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Omen in the Trees'' completed

### Meet up with the people who are awake

> *With Black Swan's help, you woke up from the illusion woven by Sunday. however, one person's strength alone is far from enough to end the Evernight before you.*

**Black Swan:** This way, darling.

> **黑天鹅：**这边，亲爱的。

*(Approach Acheron and Black Swan)*

**Acheron:** Not another one...

> **黄泉：**又一个……

**Acheron:** Thank you so much, Black Swan.

> **黄泉：**太好了。感激不尽，黑天鹅小姐。

**Black Swan:** Finally, I can breathe a sigh of relief.

> **黑天鹅：**这下我总算可以松一口气了。

- ▸ What happened?
- ▸ What is this place?
- ▸ The déjà vu is strong with this scene...

**Acheron:** I understand you must be confused, and we'll do our best to shed light on the situation. However, before that, it's essential to know that...

> **黄泉：**我知道你现在一头雾水，我们会尽力为你说明。但讲清一切的来龙去脉之前，你必须明白一件事……

**Acheron:** This place is the rift between dream and reality. A place reserved only for those who have awakened from Ena's dream.

> **黄泉：**这里是梦境和现实的狭间，属于那些从太一之梦中清醒的人。

**Black Swan:** Do you remember Sunday's ambitious plan? He intended to harness the power of the Stellaron, the collective will of over 100,000 Oak Family members, and the desires of everyone in Penacony to usurp the Harmony and restore the Order.

> **黑天鹅：**还记得那位星期日的野心么？他计划利用星核、橡木家系十万余人的意志，和匹诺康尼众生的愿望，篡夺「同谐」权柄，令「秩序」重现世间。

**Acheron:** Unfortunately, it didn't stop there. From the early days of our journey into Asdana, we were already affected by the Stellaron. That strange Dreamscape where we met... Maybe it was a sign that your thoughts were beginning to drift away.

> **黄泉：**遗憾的是，真相不仅如此。早在进入阿斯德纳之初，我们就已经受到了星核的影响。回想起来，你我相遇的那片陌生梦境，或许就是思绪开始游离的预兆。

- ▸ All of this is just a dream?
- ▸ Did I fall asleep on the Express?
- ▸ I'm now back at the beginning of the story?

**Acheron:** I don't think... the goal of the Order was to put everyone into a deep sleep. Quite the contrary — they used the Stellaron to catalyze the leakage of Asdana's memoria into the material world, allowing the Dreamscape to blend with reality.

> **黄泉：**我想…「秩序」的目的并不是让所有人陷入沉睡。正相反，他们利用星核，是为了催化阿斯德纳的忆质渗入物质世界，让「梦境」与「现实」交融。

**Acheron:** People think they are awake, but their spirits have long since stepped into the temple of Order.

> **黄泉：**人们自以为清醒，精神却早已步入「秩序」的殿堂。

**Black Swan:** This is what makes Ena's Dream so powerful. In this paradise governed by the Order, everyone indulges in their delightful dreams and lives happily ever after.

> **黑天鹅：**这也正是「太一之梦」的可怕之处。在「秩序」支配的乐园里，每个人都能获得各自美满的梦境，幸福快乐地生活下去。

**Black Swan:** I believe what you experienced in the sweet dream, except for that flaw, was real. Only in this way could you reach the destination, lifting the crisis in Penacony and embarking on your next trailblazing expedition.

> **黑天鹅：**我想，除了那唯一的一处破绽，你在美梦中经历的仍是真实发生过的。唯有如此，他才能让你抵达理想的终点：解救匹诺康尼的危机，踏上下一段「开拓」之旅。

**Black Swan:** If it wasn't for Acheron's plan, we might have been trapped in this dream forever.

> **黑天鹅：**若非黄泉小姐提前布局，我们或许就要永远沉沦在这场梦中了。

**Black Swan:** Well, I'm not as fortunate as she is. Even if I'm a Memokeeper, I was still influenced by the power of the Order and fell into hallucinations.

> **黑天鹅：**但我就没这么幸运了，即便身为忆者，也还是会受到「秩序」的影响，陷入幻觉。

**Black Swan:** However, thanks to your memories, now we still have a chance to turn the tide.

> **黑天鹅：**不过，多亏了你的「记忆」，我们现在还有绝处逢生的机会。

**Acheron:** We still have a chance, though. To make it happen... please, Black Swan, guide us to those with a strong will.

> **黄泉：**现在，我们还有一线生机。而要将它付诸现实…黑天鹅小姐，带我们去见见那些同样坚强的人吧。

**Acheron:** The truth of the Dreamscape runs deeper. We must notify anyone willing to wake up, so we can end the long night together.

> **黄泉：**梦境的真相不止于此，我们必须告知所有愿意醒来的人，才能携手终结长夜。

**Black Swan:** Alright, please come with me.

> **黑天鹅：**各位，跟我来吧。

*(Floating text)*

Blessed are they that slumber!

Thou hast answered me! Yea, answered me!

Thou art with us, united in paradise!

We abide by thy divine will!

This is our promised land!

It is done! Done it is!

I shall follow after thee!

We are delivered!

*(Approach the lobby)*

**Dan Heng:** Stay calm...

> **丹恒：**冷静点……

*(Approach Boothill, Dan Heng, and Aventurine)*

**Aventurine:** I bet that even if I don't resist, you'd still...

> **砂金：**我敢打赌，就算我不反抗，你也……

**Aventurine:** Time to get down to business, cowboy.

> **砂金：**看来该忙正事了，牛仔。

**Dan Heng:** (Trailblazer), you're okay!

> **丹恒：**开拓者，你没事。

- ▸ It's so great to see you.

**Dan Heng:** Me too.

> **丹恒：**我也一样。

- ▸ What about the others?

**Dan Heng:** A Memokeeper is looking for them. We must do everything we can as well.

> **丹恒：**一位忆者正在寻找他们，我们也必须尽己所能。

**Aventurine:** Quite the fateful encounter we have here, friend. Seems you're also a light sleeper. I'll send you some of the IPC's sleep aids one of these days.

> **砂金：**我们缘分不浅啊，朋友——看来你也睡得不沉，改天送你些公司的助眠产品。

**Black Swan:** Unfortunately, we need to stay awake right now rather than sleep. We're still nowhere near capable of breaking free from Ena's Dream.

> **黑天鹅：**可惜，现在我们更应该醒来，而非睡去。仅仅凭借我们，也还完全没有破除「太一之梦」的能力。

**Black Swan:** Let me break the bad news first. The enemy we're up against isn't just Sunday anymore, nor is it the Harmonious Choir that he's usurped.

> **黑天鹅：**先说一个坏消息吧，我们要面对的敌人，已不再仅仅是星期日了，也不再是他篡夺的「齐响诗班」。

**Black Swan:** The Dreammaster has awakened the Swarm Disaster within the sweet dream, and cast shadows into the hearts of all dreamers through Ena's Dream. The primal fear taking root in people's hearts had led them to call out to the Order for protection.

> **黑天鹅：**「梦主」在美梦中唤醒了寰宇蝗灾，并随着太一之梦，在每个入梦者的内心投下了阴影。根植在人们心中的原始恐惧，使他们开始呼唤「秩序」的庇护。

**Black Swan:** The Path of Order is gradually being restored, and this... has transformed Sunday into Ena.

> **黑天鹅：**「秩序」的命途正在逐渐复兴，而这…使星期日化身为了「太一」。

- ▸ What? That's possible?
- ▸ The Aeon of Order?

**Acheron:** Specifically, he's just one step away from becoming an Aeon. The IPC's starships converged here at an opportune moment, and the power of the Preservation seeped into the sweet dream to prevent him from completely ascending. However, that is only temporary.

> **黄泉：**准确来说，还差一步之遥。公司的星舰恰恰在此时集结，「存护」的力量渗入美梦，让他无法彻底登神，但这只是暂时的。

**Aventurine:** With the IPC around, the cosmos doesn't need a second "Order".

> **砂金：**有星际和平公司在，银河间不再需要第二种「秩序」。

**Acheron:** But it doesn't make much of a difference for us.

> **黄泉：**但对我们来说，区别不大。

**Acheron:** Sunday's true form has broken free from the Dreamscape, becoming as vast as an Aeon. The Asdana system is no more than a plaything in the palm of his hand.

> **黄泉：**星期日的正身已然脱离梦境，如星神般庞大，阿斯德纳星系也只是他的掌中之物。

**Black Swan:** Indeed. Forget about stopping him. We can't even manage to face him again.

> **黑天鹅：**是啊，别说阻止，我们甚至做不到重新面对他。

- ▸ There must be a way to counter him.
- ▸ He's got to have a weakness, right?

**Acheron:** The key still lies in Ena's Dream. Fear and sweet dreams... He relies on these two lies to gather the dreamers' "shared wishes." He's still just a "false god" who barely managed to ascend.

> **黄泉：**关键仍然在于「太一之梦」。恐惧和美梦…依靠两种谎言，他们才能汇集入梦者的「同愿」。归根结底，只是勉强扬升的「伪神」。

**Acheron:** If we can wake people up and break Ena's Dream, Sunday's divinity will fall with it.

> **黄泉：**只要能够让人们醒来，将太一之梦破除，星期日的神格也将随之陨落。

**Acheron:** But the current sweet dream is extremely complex. Miss Black Swan, could you please explain the rest of it? My memory... isn't the best, after all.

> **黄泉：**但眼下的美梦极为复杂，黑天鹅女士，接下来的情况，能麻烦你来进行解释吗？我毕竟…记性不太好。

**Black Swan:** Time is of the essence, so I'll cut to the chase. There are "three layers" to the dreamscape trapping us.

> **黑天鹅：**时间紧迫，我就长话短说了——困住我们的梦境，足有「三重」。

**Black Swan:** First, there's Ena's Dream controlled by Sunday. It wove a beautiful illusion for every dreamer, making them flounder in it.

> **黑天鹅：**其一，是由星期日掌握的「太一之梦」，它为每一个入梦者编织了美好的幻觉，使人们沉沦其中。

**Black Swan:** Second, there's the Primordial Dreamscape, which includes Dreamflux Reef. The Swarm Disaster is manifesting again in the "Oak Dream" within this layer and is continuously empowering the Order through an endless stream of fear.

> **黑天鹅：**其二，是包括流梦礁在内的「原始梦境」，寰宇蝗灾也是在此处的「橡木之梦」中重现，正以恐惧源源不绝地壮大「秩序」。

**Black Swan:** Third, there's the Twelve Hours established by the Family. This layer was the first to merge with reality and is itself an enormous trap.

> **黑天鹅：**其三，是由家族建立的「十二时刻」，这里最先与现实开始融合，本身就是一个巨大的陷阱。

**Black Swan:** Forget about winning. We need to cross all three layers of the dreamscape before we can even face Sunday again.

> **黑天鹅：**且先不说如何取胜，只有依次将三重梦境全部跨越，我们才能再次直面星期日。

- ▸ Have we woken up from the first layer yet?
- ▸ Is it really that simple?

**Black Swan:** Unfortunately, we're still in the first layer, which is Ena's Dream. Although we've regained our sense of self, we're still dreaming... We've only managed to connect our individual dreamscapes, but have not escaped.

> **黑天鹅：**很可惜，我们甚至仍在第一重，也就是「太一之梦」。虽然寻回了自我，但仍在做梦——我们只是串联起了各自的梦境，并非已然逃脱。

**Black Swan:** To destroy it, we must make everyone in Penacony "want to wake up from their dreams."

> **黑天鹅：**想要将其摧毁，我们就必须让匹诺康尼的所有人，「想要从梦中醒来」。

- ▸ How do we do that?
- ▸ Reality sure is a force to be reckoned with...

**Black Swan:** Humans' yearning for sweet illusions borders on obsession, leading them to subconsciously resist... the harsh reality.

> **黑天鹅：**人对美好幻觉的向往近乎偏执，这种心理会令人下意识地抗拒…残酷的真相。

**Black Swan:** Therefore, I carefully selected a moment where (Trailblazer) was completely unguarded, guiding him/her to uncover the truth himself/herself to make him/her regain his/her consciousness.

> **黑天鹅：**我也是费心挑选了一个开拓者全无防备的时刻，引导他/她亲自揭开真相，才得以让他/她取回清醒。

**Black Swan:** However, to wake up everyone in Penacony and get them to share the same determination... that would be nearly impossible.

> **黑天鹅：**但匹诺康尼的所有人…想让如此规模的人群产生相同的意志，恐怕难如登天。

**Dan Heng:** But we can't just stay here and do nothing.

> **丹恒：**但无论如何，我们都无法坐视不理。

**Acheron:** This is the first step of our plan. With the assistance from the Garden of Recollection, those who possess a strong will, like you, will gradually awaken from the dream. These free wills are the "discord" that will sway Ena's Dream.

> **黄泉：**这是计划的第一步。在流光忆庭的帮助下，像各位一样足够坚强的人会逐渐从梦中清醒，苏醒的自由意志会成为撼动太一之梦的「不协和音」。

**Dan Heng:** However, awakening a mere handful of individuals is insignificant compared to the vast number of people immersed in the dream. We must find other ways to awaken the free will of millions of people within a short period of time.

> **丹恒：**但寥寥数十人，在如此庞大的梦境面前无异于沧海一粟。我们必须寻找其他办法，在短时间内唤醒以十万、百万为单位的自由意志。

**Dan Heng:** ...

> **丹恒：**……

**Dan Heng:** Looks like the Jade Abacus of Allying Oath will be the only solution.

> **丹恒：**想来，也只有「结盟玉兆」能做到了。

**Boothill:** ...No, no need for that.

> **波提欧：**——不，没这个必要。

**Boothill:** Keep your once-in-a-lifetime treasure. We don't need to bother the Xianzhou Alliance for such a tiny request. You want thousands of people with unwavering free will? Hah, that's easy...

> **波提欧：**犯不着惊动仙舟联盟，一辈子只能用一次的宝贝你好好留着吧。成千上万的自由意志？小意思——

**Boothill:** Just leave it to us Galaxy Rangers.

> **波提欧：**交给「巡海游侠」来解决。

**Boothill:** Do you know what it is, Acheron?

> **波提欧：**黄泉，知道这是什么吗？

**Acheron:** It's the relic I returned to you.

> **黄泉：**是我交还给你的「遗物」。

**Boothill:** Exactly. Its owner must have told you that it's meaningless to anyone other than a Galaxy Ranger, and that it can only fulfill its purpose when returned to its rightful owner.

> **波提欧：**对。它的主人一定告诉过你，这东西对巡海游侠以外的人一文不值，只有物归原主才能发挥作用。

**Boothill:** Because it's a burial artifact, worthy only of a hero who has served the Galaxy Rangers with honor. When its light illuminates the universe, it means the fall of a hero, and in the direction it falls...

> **波提欧：**因为这是一件随葬品，只有为巡海游侠立下赫赫战功的英雄才配拥有。当它的光芒出现在宇宙，也就意味着一颗巨星的陨落，而它落下的方向……

**Boothill:** Countless meteors will streak across the sky. Those "meteors" are Galaxy Rangers coming from all corners of the Cosmos, driven by a shared purpose, without questioning the cause or counting the cost. Because we abide by a bottom line...

> **波提欧：**会有无数流星划破天际，那是巡海游侠集结的火光。他们从银河四方赶来，不问缘由，不计代价，只因我们遵守一条共同的底线——

**Boothill:** The shooting stars of The Hunt only descend on the longest night, and with them... comes the dawn.

> **波提欧：**「巡猎」的飞星，只会坠落在最漫长的夜晚，而在它身后——将是黎明的到来。

**Boothill:** We've stayed silent for far too long. Now, it's time to remind all the cowards, oppressors, and villains of the universe of our presence! I'll be the one to ignite the first spark.

> **波提欧：**我们已经沉寂了太久。是时候让全宇宙的懦夫、蛀虫和压迫者重新想起巡海游侠的名字了，就由我来打响第一枪。

**Black Swan:** This should be enough to delay his ascension to godhood. As for the foundation of Ena's Dream, it would take an immense power to shake it in an instant.

> **黑天鹅：**这足以延缓他登神的进展，至于太一之梦的根基，则需要极为强大的力量，在刹那间加以动摇。

**Acheron:** I think... That shouldn't be too hard.

> **黄泉：**我想…这也不难。

**Black Swan:** So, you're the key in the end, I assume?

> **黑天鹅：**所以，最终还是回到了你这边，是吗？

**Black Swan:** Even if it is just a counterfeit god, we'll need an Emanator to step in.

> **黑天鹅：**即便只是赝作，也非得由一位令使出手不可。

**Acheron:** Perhaps it's not about being an Emanator... This is a different "collective wish." I will do everything in my power... to strike down that evil god for you all.

> **黄泉：**或许与令使的身份无关…这是迥然不同的「众愿」，我将倾尽所能——为你们斩落恶神。

**Dan Heng:** What about the other two dreamscape layers?

> **丹恒：**剩下的两重梦境呢？

**Acheron:** There's nothing we can do about them. They're even more dangerous.

> **黄泉：**无可奈何，甚至更加危险。

**Acheron:** Unlike our current gathering, the dissipation of Ena's Dream will take time, which will draw Sunday's attention.

> **黄泉：**不同于此刻的集结——太一之梦的消散需要时间，足以引起星期日的注意。

**Black Swan:** Exactly. Once we enter the next layer of the Dreamscape, we'll be facing an Aeon's strength head-on. But by then, we'll have played all our cards.

- ▸ Can't we wait for the others to wake up?

**Black Swan:** Ena's Dream is no ordinary matter. Even with Constance and me giving it our all, we could only reach the few of you here. It's impossible to find more help.

> **黑天鹅：**是啊，前往下一重梦境后，我们就要直面星神的力量。但在那之前，我们已经出完了全部底牌。

**Black Swan:** As for beyond the sweet dream, our only hope lies in intervention from other Aeons... But that would be tantamount to igniting a cosmic war among gods.

> **黑天鹅：**太一之梦也绝非等闲，我和康士坦丝倾尽全力，也只能寻得眼前的寥寥诸位，再难有其他援手。

**Black Swan:** The Order is one of Xipe's aspects, yet the Harmony stands idle at this moment as Ena is being reborn, without any further intervention.... There must be some deeper reason behind this.

> **黑天鹅：**至于美梦之外，只能指望其他星神进行干涉——而那无异于掀起一场寰宇神战。

- ▸ Won't anyone on the outside notice?

**Black Swan:** It's too late. Unless another Aeon intervenes... But that would be tantamount to igniting a cosmic war among gods.

> **黑天鹅：**来不及了，除非有其他星神进行干涉——而那无异于掀起一场寰宇神战。

**Black Swan:** The Order is one of Xipe's aspects, yet the Harmony stands idle at this moment as Ena is being reborn, without any further intervention.... There must be some deeper reason behind this.

> **黑天鹅：**「秩序」是希佩的一重面相，此刻，「同谐」却坐视「太一」重诞，没有更进一步的干涉——这其中必然有着极深的隐情。

**Black Swan:** Ena's Dream is no ordinary matter. Even with Constance and me giving it our all, we could only reach the few of you here. It's impossible to find more help.

> **黑天鹅：**太一之梦也绝非等闲，我和康士坦丝倾尽全力，也只能寻得眼前的寥寥诸位，再难有其他援手。

**Acheron:** But time is of the essence. We must take our fate into our own hands.

> **黄泉：**但时机稍纵即逝，我们必须自救。

**Aventurine:** In other words, those who only want to survive by playing it safe will die, while those who dare to face death head-on will live. I like that.

> **砂金：**说白了，想要苟且偷生的人总会死去，敢面对死亡的人才能活下来——我喜欢。

**Silver Wolf:** Just these few people? Oh well, that'll do.

> **银狼：**就这么几个人？哦，也够了。

- ▸ Oh, you're here too?
- ▸ Minors are not allowed.

**Silver Wolf:** Quiet down. You're just a kid yourself.

> **银狼：**别叫，想想你自己才多大。

**Silver Wolf:** It's not just Penacony. The entire Asdana system is done for.

> **银狼：**不光匹诺康尼，整个阿斯德纳星系全完了。

**Dan Heng:** ...A Stellaron Hunter?

> **丹恒：**…星核猎手？

**The Dahlia:** She was at the edge of the Asdana system, but she still got dragged into this mess. Just finding her nearly wiped me out.

> **大丽花：**她身处阿斯德纳星系边缘，但还是被卷了进来，只是找到她一个人，我几乎就要油尽灯枯了。

**Aventurine:** Quite a bold investment strategy! Tell me, with such a huge risk, what kind of payoff are we talking about?

> **砂金：**豪迈的投资风格！说说看吧——这么大的风险，能带来什么收获？

**Silver Wolf:** The other two layers of the dreamscape will be handled by the Stellaron Hunters.

> **银狼：**另外两重梦境，星核猎手来搞定。

**Silver Wolf:** One of our associates is searching in the Twelve Hours for the other person chosen by the Harmony.

> **银狼：**我们的一位合作者，正在十二时刻寻找另一个「同谐」神选。

**Aventurine:** Oh? Miss Robin?

> **砂金：**哦？知更鸟小姐？

**Silver Wolf:** She broke free from Ena's Dream through her own strength and left behind a "song" for those in slumber.

> **银狼：**她靠自己的力量挣脱了太一之梦，而且给沉睡的人们，留下了一支「歌」。

**Robin:** It's true that some people are born strong, and others are born weak. If the Trailblaze is the target of heroes, then the Harmony will guarantee that the strong help the weak. Only the people of Penacony themselves can be the saviors of their homeland.

> **知更鸟：**人固然有强大与弱小之分，倘若「开拓」是英雄的使命，那么「同谐」的责任便是以强援弱，因为匹诺康尼的救世主，只能是匹诺康尼人自己。

**Robin:** Their path of happiness should be forged by themselves. While I may not be a Nameless, I'm willing to instill courage in all those who need it.

> **知更鸟：**每个人的幸福和道路应当由他自己开创。虽然我不是无名客，但也愿意将飞上天空的勇气传递给每一个需要它的人。

**Robin:** This includes my brother as well. Ena's Dream... is too cruel for him, and everyone else.

> **知更鸟：**这其中也包括我的哥哥。太一之梦…对他、对所有人都太过残酷了。

**Black Swan:** I see... So we were able to wake up thanks to Miss Robin's help. But... The flaws rooted in human nature can't be eradicated overnight. Do you believe that these efforts alone are enough to convince everyone to choose the right path?

> **黑天鹅：**原来如此…看来我们能够苏醒，当中也有知更鸟小姐的帮助。可是…人性的弱点绝非一朝一夕能够克服，仅凭这些努力，真的能让所有人弃暗投明么？

**Acheron:** I agree with you, Black Swan. That's why the most critical aspect of this plan is not to convince everyone to choose the right path...

> **黄泉：**诚然如此，黑天鹅小姐。所以，计划中最重要的一环不是让所有人弃暗投明……

**Acheron:** ...But to inspire them to save themselves.

> **黄泉：**——而是让所有人选择自救。

**Silver Wolf:** They'll stir up something big to help break you free from the Twelve Hours.

> **银狼：**她们会搞点大动静出来，为你们破除十二时刻。

**Silver Wolf:** As for the Primordial Dreamscape, SAM is standing by, just waiting for you to break free from Ena's Dream.

> **银狼：**至于「原始梦境」，萨姆正在待命，就等你们挣脱太一之梦了。

- ▸ Is she okay?
- ▸ What will she do?

**Silver Wolf:** Now. I'll tell you, but don't panic.

> **银狼：**听了以后，你别急。

**Silver Wolf:** She will die. She will die a second time, facing everything she wants to run away from.

> **银狼：**她会死，面对所有想要逃避的东西，第二次死去。

**Silver Wolf:** But her fate will be determined by you. She will help you break through the second layer of the dreamscape. Then she'll fall into Ena's Dream, which hasn't fully dissipated yet.

> **银狼：**但她的结局，会由你来决定。她会为你们破除第二重梦境，然后坠入还没彻底消散的太一之梦。

**Silver Wolf:** If you defeat Sunday and wake her from that dream, then her second death, too, will become nothing but an illusion.

> **银狼：**如果你战胜了星期日，让她从梦中醒来，这一次死亡，也会成为一场幻梦。

**Silver Wolf:** But if you fail, the dreamscape will ultimately merge completely with reality, and her death will become a fact.

> **银狼：**而如果失败了，梦境最终会与现实完全融合，她的死亡就会成为事实。

- ▸ I won't let her die.
- ▸ Her dream deserves a better ending.

**Black Swan:** But that still doesn't solve the fundamental problem. What about Sunday? He could wipe out all our efforts with no more than a glance the moment we cross the three dreamscape layers.

> **黑天鹅：**可是，这还是没有解决最根本的问题。星期日该怎么办？在跨越三重梦境的时刻，他只需一次注视，就能抹杀我们全部的努力。

**Aventurine:** Miss Memokeeper, it's time for a game of recall. I once said something to you lot back in the Oak Dream.

> **砂金：**忆者女士，回忆的时候到了——在橡木之梦里，我对你们说过一句话。

**Aventurine:** So long as my "trump card" remains unplayed, none will dare see the hand through to the end. And now, it's time to reveal that ace.

> **砂金：**只要我「底牌未出」，没有一个人敢跟注到底。现在，是揭开那张牌的时候了。

**Aventurine:** Let me point out two mistakes. One of them... is yours.

> **砂金：**我来指出两个错误吧，一个是你们的——

**Aventurine:** Having personally been to the heartland of the Dreammaster's plans, wouldn't I have already found a way to inform the IPC? The fleet's deployment was never meant for a direct confrontation.

> **砂金：**亲身到过梦主计划的腹地，难道我不会想办法告知公司？舰队开拔，一开始就不是为了正面冲突。

**Aventurine:** It was to seed the Preservation's heart into the dream.

> **砂金：**那是为了给梦中的「存护」之心，打下根基。

**Aventurine:** The other mistake was Sunday's, and it was a fatal one. He should never have confiscated my Cornerstone. He should have kicked me out right from the start.

> **砂金：**另一个，则是星期日的错误，而且绝对致命——他不该收缴「基石」，而应该一开始就把我赶出去。

**Aventurine:** That's how every gamble with me goes. The moment you sat down at my table... You've already lost.

> **砂金：**和我进行的赌局总是如此——你坐上赌桌的那一刻，就已经输了。

**Aventurine:** A jade-colored light still sleeps deep within the dreamscape, and it does not require the presence of its owner to unleash its powers.

> **砂金：**一道「翡翠」色的光芒，仍然沉于梦境的深处。而它的解放，不需要持有者在场。

**The Dahlia:** So what? Even the power of the Ten Stonehearts can't rattle an existence already approaching Aeonhood.

> **大丽花：**在此之后呢？石心十人的力量，也不可能撼动接近星神的存在。

**Aventurine:** That's why I said the cosmos no longer needs a second Order. The Dreammaster's plan is indeed flawless... However, when faced with the terror of the Swarm Disaster, how is calling for the Preservation any different from calling for the Order?

> **砂金：**所以我才会说：银河间不再需要第二种「秩序」。梦主的计划确实天衣无缝…然而，面对虫灾的恐惧，呼唤「存护」，难道有何不同吗？

**Aventurine:** The release of the Cornerstone's powers will absorb the dreamers' desires and offer them the Preservation's promise instead, guiding them to cry out these words... All for the Amber Lord!

> **砂金：**基石的解放将会汲取入梦者的「欲望」，给出「存护」的许诺，引领他们如此呼唤——一切献给，琥珀王！

**Dan Heng:** Is this the IPC's real goal? To completely take back Penacony on a conceptual Path level?

> **丹恒：**这才是公司真正的意图？从命途的层面，完全夺回匹诺康尼？

**Aventurine:** What, you think I'm also trampling over others' beliefs? Heh. Sorry, but that's exactly what the IPC believes in: Don't question your own ruthlessness. All that matters is profit.

> **砂金：**怎么，觉得我也在轻视别人的信仰吗？巧了，这就是公司的信仰——别怀疑自己冷酷无情，重要的是每股收益。

**Aventurine:** The new Swarm Disaster will end in the same way as it ended before. The Hammer Strike will echo throughout Asdana, seizing the Order's powers on the level of Paths to launch our counter-attack, leaving Sunday powerless to interfere with your actions.

> **砂金：**寰宇蝗灾曾经是如何结束，现在也将如何结束。「锤音」将响彻阿斯德纳，从命途层面夺取「秩序」的力量，进行反击，让星期日无力顾及你们的行动。

**Aventurine:** But a new Amber Era will not arrive because of that. Today's Hammer Strike is not the wrath of an Aeon, but humanity's battle cry of defiance.

> **砂金：**但琥珀纪不会因此翻开下一页——今日的锤击，不是星神的怒火，而是人反击的号声。

**Acheron:** ...

> **黄泉：**……

**Acheron:** I'll hold my breath and listen for that moment.

> **黄泉：**那一刻，我会屏息聆听。

**The Dahlia:** Once our plan succeeds, we can strip Sunday of his godhood, but we'll be too drained to join the battle afterward. So...

> **大丽花：**计划一旦成功，我们就能剥夺星期日的神格，但也无力参与此后的战斗。所以……

- ▸ I'll defeat him!
- ▸ I'll wake everyone up!
- ▸ I will give Penacony a tomorrow!
- ▸ I will keep trailblazing forward!

**Acheron:** Take your time to prepare. Before you head out, may I have a moment alone with you, (Trailblazer)? There's one more thing I need to explain to you.

### Step onto the final stage

*(Talk to Acheron)*

**Acheron:** This grand festival is drawing to its close. This is the starting point for the ultimate stage of our journey... just as it marked the beginning of all the stories in Penacony.

> **黄泉：**这场盛大的宴会快要结束了。这里便是前往最后舞台的起点…也曾是匹诺康尼所有故事的起点。

**Acheron:** ...Are you ready?

- ▸ Head to where the story began.

**Acheron:** That's alright. Take your time to contemplate this moment. Once you feel ready, let's step onto the final stage.

> **黄泉：**很好。那么，请闭上眼睛吧……

Robin's memories are slowly emerging...

Back to Robin's POV, even after breaking free from Ena's Dream, the crisis she faces is far from over...

Ena's Dream, the eternal high noon...

**Robin:** Am I... awake?

> **知更鸟：**我…醒过来了吗？

**Sunday:** Sorry, Robin, it's just you... I did not wish for you to know this. It's a pity that things have turned out this way.

> **星期日：**对不起，知更鸟，唯独你…我不想你知道这一切。可惜，事与愿违。

**Sunday:** Within the foundation of law, humanity establishes civilization...

> **星期日：**于律法之中，人类构建社会……

**Sunday:** And through Harmony, we obtain Order.

> **星期日：**于「同谐」之中，我们拥获「秩序」。

**Robin:** My brother... Did you still bring the Order's sweet dream into our paradise?

> **知更鸟：**哥哥，你还是把「秩序」的美梦…带给了我们的乐园？

**Sparkle:** Oww, you're still trying to play the family card at this hour? Your brother doesn't exist anymore. As of now, you'd better call THEM... Ena.

> **花火：**都这会了，就别攀关系啦——你的哥哥，已经不存在了哦？现在，你应该叫祂「太一」。

**Sparkle:** Impressive! You really woke up on your own!

> **花火：**哎呀，真了不起，你还真的自己醒过来啦？

**Robin:** Let me reiterate. If you've had your fill, it'd be wise to leave before it's too late. The music of the Harmony doesn't tolerate discord.

> **知更鸟：**我再重申一次——如果你玩够了，那还是趁早离开为好。「同谐」的乐章不能容忍杂音。

**Sparkle:** Owwww. Then I'll reiterate as well. I've done my part, and now I'm simply waiting for the fireworks to begin.

> **花火：**哎呀，那我也重申一次——我该做的事都做完了，接下来就只等看烟花咯。

**Robin:** Why don't you just cut to the chase?

> **知更鸟：**你不妨有话直说。

**Sparkle:** Time's short, so I'll condense the report. Oho! That even rhymed! I'm so talented~!

> **花火：**时间不多，长话短说——呀，还押韵了呢，我真棒！

**Sparkle:** A nosy Cremator left something on you. She wanted to use it to burn down the illusions you received from the sweet dream, allowing you to wake up from Ena's Dream... Buuut it looks like you don't need it anymore.

> **花火：**有个多管闲事的焚化工，她在你身上留了个小玩意儿，想要用它烧掉美梦给你的幻景，让你能从太一之梦里醒过来——但现在看来，你也用不上嘛。

**Robin:** ...Then, how were you able to reach this place?

> **知更鸟：**…那么，你又为何能出现在这里？

**Sparkle:** Well...

> **花火：**这个嘛……

**Sparkle:** A fool has thousands of faces. It's gonna take a lot to create a sweet dream that can satisfy me.

> **花火：**愚者千面，能让我满意的美梦，一时半会可编不出来。

**Sparkle:** Both you and I... can still stay awake for just a little bit longer.

> **花火：**你和我…我们都还能最后清醒一小会儿。

**Robin:** Just a little bit...?

> **知更鸟：**只是一会儿吗……

**Sparkle:** Honestly, I don't even need to find you, but I am just sooo kind. I've already killed you once to give you a chance to regain your home field advantage.

> **花火：**实话说，我根本用不着来找你的，但谁叫花火大人心地善良呢？我杀过你一次，想给你个机会找回场子。

**Sparkle:** You're the last "festival host" awake in Penacony. So, do you want to play a round of roulette? The wager will be the entire Planet of Festivities.

> **花火：**匹诺康尼还醒着的「东道主」，就剩你一个啦，所以，想不想来玩一场轮盘游戏——赌注是整个盛会之星。

**Robin:** Stay where you are! What are you trying to do by showing up in front of me?

> **知更鸟：**最好别轻举妄动——你来到我面前，究竟想干什么？

**Sparkle:** I want you to watch me blow up all of Penacony. Only then can everyone else launch a counterattack and wake up together.

> **花火：**想让你看着我把整个匹诺康尼炸上天。然后，其他人才能开始反击，一块儿醒过来。

**Sparkle:** Think about it. Your brother is already a god beyond the heavens. You're not gonna even see him unless you rip open the sky.

> **花火：**想想看，你哥哥已经是天空外的神了。不把天空撕开个口子，可是连面都见不着。

**Sparkle:** Ever heard of the trolley problem? Make your choice. Do you want everyone to go down together, or to have Penacony make a "small sacrifice"?

> **花火：**电车难题听说过没？选一个吧——大家一起玩完，还是让匹诺康尼付出点「小小的牺牲」？

**Robin:** Why should I trust the words of a Masked Fool?

> **知更鸟：**我为何要相信一名假面愚者的说辞？

**Sparkle:** Don't think that I'm just trolling you. My bombs are hidden all over Penacony. They took a really long time to set up, you know.

> **花火：**别以为我在吓唬你，匹诺康尼到处是我埋下的炸弹，花了不少功夫呢。

**Sparkle:** Teehee~ Maybe I should blow up a few to demonstrate how they look? Lemme see... which ones should I choose...

> **花火：**嘻嘻，要不要我先引爆几颗，让你先看看效果？我瞧瞧哪边的……

**Robin:** ...Stop.

> **知更鸟：**…停手吧。

**Robin:** What's gonna happen to the dreamers... once the bombs go off?

> **知更鸟：**在你那样做之后…入梦者会怎么样？

**Sparkle:** They will all die.

> **花火：**全都得死。

**Robin:** ...Then you know I'd never agree to it.

> **知更鸟：**…那你应该知道，我不可能同意。

**Sparkle:** When it's time to make a sacrifice, reality won't be giving you any choices. Or do you want to replay the old joke about the Harmony treating everyone the same?

> **花火：**该取舍的时候，现实可由不得你。难道你还想再来一遍「同谐」一视同仁的笑话？

**Robin:** "Life" is never a joke, Fool. There is no such thing as a lesser evil in this world. Evil, just like morality, cannot be measured, nor can it be compared.

> **知更鸟：**「生命」在任何时候都不是笑话，愚者。这个世界上从不存在次要的恶行，它和道德一样，不能被丈量，也不能用来对比。

**Sparkle:** Isn't that still the Harmony's same old song and dance? But that suits me just fine. Didn't I already say that this is a round of roulette, and not an ammunition showcase?

> **花火：**那不还是「同谐」那一套嘛，不过，这倒正合我意——我不是说了嘛，这是个轮盘游戏，不是爆破表演。

**Sparkle:** Get ready to sing. Be like a bird. If you can make Xipe hear you, then THEY definitely won't allow me to blow everyone to pieces, right?

> **花火：**准备歌唱吧，就像一只小鸟。如果你能让那位希佩听见，祂也不会看着我炸死所有人的，对吧？

**Sparkle:** What will you choose now? You can either watch as I blow the entire place up, or you can try to save their lives.

> **花火：**现在你会怎么选？要不眼睁睁看着我炸飞这里，要么就试试看，至少能救他们的命。

**Sparkle:** Maybe this will be your last ever performance.

> **花火：**说不定，这就是你最后一场演出了哦？

**Robin:** Last ever performance...

> **知更鸟：**最后一次……

**Siobhan:** ...You feeling nervous? I know the feeling. You should feel nervous when going up on such a big stage for the first time.

> **舒翁：**…怎么，紧张了？也是，第一次登上这么隆重的舞台，就该紧张些。

**Robin:** ...I'm okay. I just remembered something, that's all.

> **知更鸟：**…还好，只是回忆起了一些事。

**Robin:** Miss Siobhan... Why do you think birds fly?

> **知更鸟：**舒翁女士，在你看来，鸟为什么要飞上天空？

**Siobhan:** ...Why're you asking that all of a sudden?

> **舒翁：**…怎么突然问起这个？

**Robin:** That was my response as well the first time I heard that question... It's what my brother asked me before I left Penacony. I keep finding myself thinking about it.

> **知更鸟：**第一次听到的时候，我也这么想——离开匹诺康尼前，哥哥就是这么问我的，我总是忍不住要思考。

**Siobhan:** I don't know. I fly when it's time to fly, and I stop when I don't want to fly anymore. There's no reason.

> **舒翁：**不知道，我该飞时就飞，不想飞了就下来，没有为什么。

**Robin:** ...It seems that your answer is really similar to mine.

> **知更鸟：**…那舒翁女士的答案，会和我的很像呢。

**Robin:** You've also asked me "why do I sing." I think I can answer both of these questions in one go.

> **知更鸟：**您也问过我「因何而唱」，我想，这两个问题，我可以一起回答。

**Robin:** The answer wasn't what I initially had in mind. Originally, I wanted to sing because of something specific that I experienced...

> **知更鸟：**最初，我的想法还不是这样，我想要歌唱，是因为一个特殊的契机——

**Robin:** A Stellaron fell onto my homeworld when I was young. Everything I knew was instantly destroyed. We didn't even get a chance to react to it. No matter what we did, it was too late.

> **知更鸟：**在我还小的时候，星核落入了我的故乡，眼前一切顷刻毁败，甚至没有给我们留下任何反应的空间，无论做什么，都为时已晚。

**Robin:** But for some reason, my mother chose to hum a song when she tried to comfort me and my brother.

> **知更鸟：**不知为何，母亲试着安抚我和哥哥时，却选择了哼唱一段曲调。

**Siobhan:** I remember that story. Everyone thought that's why you decided to sing.

> **舒翁：**我也记得。而且，大家都认为，你是因此才选择了歌唱。

**Robin:** No... That experience only made me detest singing.

> **知更鸟：**不——这段经历只会让我憎恨歌唱。

**Robin:** I've believed for a long time that songs can't change anything when disasters strike... That music is the most useless thing in this world.

> **知更鸟：**长久以来，我都是这样认为：灾难发生时，一首歌改变不了任何事，它简直是世界上最没用的东西。

**Robin:** I might be extraordinarily talented in singing, and I might feel super happy whenever I sang... But its futility had always made me depressed.

> **知更鸟：**尽管我拥有出众的音律天赋，也会发自内心，为歌唱时的每一个瞬间感到喜悦…但它的「无用」，也总是令我感到惆怅。

**Siobhan:** ...Then, what changed?

> **舒翁：**…后来呢？

**Robin:** I grew up, and I realized that my brother would sometimes hum the same tune as my mother did. And I thought maybe he missed her, but I was too embarrassed to point that out. I tried to comfort him instead.

> **知更鸟：**我渐渐长大，有时会发现，哥哥也会时不时哼唱同一段曲调，那时，我以为他在想念妈妈，却又不好意思直说，想要去安慰他。

**Robin:** However, his answer to me made me realize what I should be singing for...

> **知更鸟：**而他的回答，让我知道了，自己该因何而唱——

**Robin:** Obviously, he didn't give me an answer directly, but it got me to think... Maybe some things don't need a reason or meaning at all?

> **知更鸟：**当然，他并没有直接给我答案，但却开始让我思考…有些事，是否根本不需要理由和意义？

**Robin:** Go and do what must be done, Fool. It's for the sake of Penacony's future... But I won't let anyone get hurt.

> **知更鸟：**为了匹诺康尼的未来，放手去做吧，愚者——但我不会让任何人受到伤害。

**Sparkle:** ...Ooh, did you just have a flashback? Now I'm even more excited!

> **花火：**…呀，你刚才是不是突然回忆杀了？更期待了呢。

**Sparkle:** Lemme check one last time. If you were to die, how would you want people to remember your ending?

> **花火：**最后一次了，万一你死掉，想让人们怎么回忆你的结局？

**Sparkle:** "Like a bird, she lived her life, graceful and composed. Now her beak parts slightly with a sigh, as soft as falling feathers." Like it?

> **花火：**「她一生如鸟，优雅从容，此刻微启唇喙，一声惋叹，轻如羽落」——喜欢吗？

**Robin:** Drop the sanctimonious mask. Singing only needs one thing, and that's "sincerity."

> **知更鸟：**收起你的虚饰吧，歌唱需要的，只有「真挚」。

**Robin:** My brother... Birds born with wings need no reason to soar into the sky.

> **知更鸟：**哥哥，生来拥有翅膀的鸟儿，飞上天际时不需要理由。

**Robin:** And those forced to "fall back to earth" don't need anyone's condescending sympathy.

> **知更鸟：**而被迫「坠落于地」的，也不需要高高在上的同情。

**Robin:** The sky and earth are each other's "proof" and "aspiration"...

> **知更鸟：**天空与大地，是彼此的「证明」与「向往」——

**Robin:** When a soaring bird looks down below, it discovers that even after falling, life still holds hope to continue.

> **知更鸟：**飞翔的鸟儿低头俯视，会发现坠落之后，生命也仍有希望持续。

**Robin:** And when a fallen bird looks up to the sky, it discovers that the possibility of soaring once again still exists in this world.

> **知更鸟：**坠落的鸟儿抬头仰望，会发现再度飞翔的可能，仍然存在于这世界上。

**Robin:** Perhaps they'll never truly understand each other, but what people need isn't understanding. It's showing a little more compassion for the way others choose to live, and when necessary, extending a helping hand...

> **知更鸟：**或许他们永远不会理解彼此，但人们需要的从来不是理解，而是在他人选择的生存方式面前，多一点点体谅，必要时，也伸出援手——

**Robin:** Even if you become the only star and prevent everyone from soaring into the sky... they will still be inspired by your radiance, flap their wings with all their might, regardless of whether they've been hurt in their fall.

> **知更鸟：**即使你成为了唯一的星，阻止所有人飞上天际——他们也会因你的光芒，产生向往，奋力拍打自己的翅膀，无论是否曾在坠落时受伤。

**Robin:** Thus, the Order's sweet dream will eventually fall. O, Triple-Faced God... If I manage to step onto the Path of Harmony, then please extend your hand and shelter those who believe in you.

> **知更鸟：**所以，「秩序」的美梦终将破灭。三重面相的神明啊…若我踏上了「同谐」的道路，请伸出援手，庇护信奉你的人们。

**Robin:** And if I am a bird destined to fall, doomed to never again sing... Then, while the sound of me hitting the ground may be my last note for the Harmony, it will never be this world's swansong!

> **知更鸟：**而如果，我是注定坠亡的鸟儿，无法再次歌唱——我摔落于地面的声音，或许会是我最后的「同谐」乐章，但却绝不会成为这个世界的绝响！

**Robin:** In the name of the sweet dream, the Charmony Festival... Now begins!

> **知更鸟：**以美梦的名义，谐乐大典，就此开幕！

**Trustful Boy:**♪～♪♪♪～

> **天真的少年：**♪～♪♪♪～

**Guileless Girl:** Brother, do you... miss home?

> **烂漫的少女：**哥哥，你…想家了吗？

**Trustful Boy:** Even if I do, it's too late.

> **天真的少年：**就算想，一切也都为时已晚。

**Guileless Girl:** But you're singing the song Mom used to sing...

> **烂漫的少女：**可是，你在唱的，是妈妈唱过的歌……

**Trustful Boy:** Because... Even if it's too late, we still have the choice to sing whatever we can.

> **天真的少年：**因为，哪怕一切为时已晚，我们还是能去唱…自己能唱的歌。

Returning to Trailblazer's POV...

When you have a chance to make a choice, make one that you know you won't regret.

At the same time: The beginning of the story, and also, the final stage

**Acheron:** Welcome to Horizon of Existence...

> **黄泉：**欢迎来到「存在的地平线」……

**Acheron:** This place is one of the thousands of manifestations belonging to the sleeping and shapeless, and it's also an exit out of the Nihility for the awake ones.

> **黄泉：**这里是沉眠无相者的万千表征之一，但同样，也是清醒之人告别「虚无」的出口。

**Acheron:** Let's bid our final farewells here.

> **黄泉：**因此，我们就在这作最后的道别吧。

**Acheron:** Remember your name. I'll be waiting for you at the end.

> **黄泉：**记住自己的名字，我会在尽头等你。

*(Unlock Achievement Flying Requires No Reason)*

### Head to the edge of Nihility

> *As the grand banquet draws to a close, all that remains is to Trailblaze forward and reclaim tomorrow for Penacony.*

Memokeeper's Wish: If only people could have each other beyond just memories.

Greed's Wish: I want everything this world has to offer, or perhaps I want to find the one existence that can truly please me.

Gambler's Wish: I hope that I'll see her again after I die. Then she'll know that I, too, am only human... And all humans will find happiness in the end.

Cowboy's Wish: I hope everyone can always reunite with their family, enjoy home-cooked stew, and keep on laughing.

Mundanite's Wish: One day, those who live will all come to understand how they need to think to create their own happiness.

Beauty's Wish: May people embrace each other, and offer their most sincere compliments to whoever they meet!

**The Wish Entrusted to You:** Our hopes and dreams are in your hands now. Please guide them to witness every sunrise that is yet to come.

*(Approach Silver Wolf's apparition)*

**Allies Against the Finality:** Hey, I've read the "script"! Why are you asking me?

- ▸ What if I fail?

**Allies Against the Finality:** Then do your best to not fail.

- ▸ Will we wake up?

**Allies Against the Finality:** As long as you're not afraid to fall asleep, you'll definitely wake up.

**Trailblazing Companions:** It's not the time to leave your final words yet!

> **一同「开拓」的同伴：**还不到留愿望的时候帕！

**Trailblazing Companions:** We all believe in you.

> **一同「开拓」的同伴：**我们都相信你。

**Trailblazing Companions:** You need to believe in us too.

> **一同「开拓」的同伴：**你也要相信我们。

**Trailblazing Companions:** The trailblazing expedition won't end here.

> **一同「开拓」的同伴：**开拓之旅不会就此结束。

**Trailblazing Companions:** We'll wake up together.

> **一同「开拓」的同伴：**我们会一起醒来。

**Acheron:** Why does life slumber...? We don't know the answer yet, but we're about to awaken from this dream.

> **黄泉：**是啊，生命因何而沉睡…我们仍未知晓这一问的答案，却已然要从这场梦中清醒。

**Acheron:** Or perhaps, such is the answer itself.

> **黄泉：**又或者，这就是答案本身。

- ▸ Those things were...?

**Acheron:** It's the people who first broke free from the sweet dream. The sleeping and shapeless caught a glimpse of their desires.

> **黄泉：**是最先从美梦中挣脱的人们——沉眠无相者瞥见了他们的愿望。

**Acheron:** It was because they held onto these desires that they could dream. It was because these desires were never fulfilled that they could wake up.

> **黄泉：**正因怀抱这些愿望，他们才有能力做梦。正因从未实现过这些愿望，他们才有能力醒来。

- ▸ So, after a wish comes true...
- ▸ ...People would feel nihilistic?

**Acheron:** I think... that's not entirely true. I wish people could find happiness, but I've been tainted by too much nihilism... Sometimes I can't help but think...

> **黄泉：**我想…并不尽然。我情愿人们能够幸福，但我沾染过太多的无…有时也不禁这样想——

**Acheron:** That the Nihility is a proud and terrifying dream. It's brought forth not by gain or loss, but by the severing of roads, or even... the loss of desire itself.

> **黄泉：**「虚无」是骄傲而又可怕的梦。招致它的，不是得到，也不是失去，而是道路的断绝，甚至…欲望的丧失。

**Acheron:** Fulfilling a wish can certainly make people feel the joy of being alive, but if no new wishes ever surfaced again in people's hearts...

> **黄泉：**实现心愿，当然能够带来生存的喜悦。但若是此后，再也没有新的愿望，浮现在人们的心间……

- ▸ ...Then they will gaze upon nihility.
- ▸ That's what the Order's sweet dream is all about.

**Acheron:** Indeed... Behind the shadow of the Nihility, there must exist the most blazing source of light in the world.

> **黄泉：**是啊…「虚无」这道影子的背后，也一定存在着世间最猛烈的光源。

**Acheron:** Just as every life that edges closer to death fervently approaches the end of the Nihility...

> **黄泉：**正如每一个迈向死亡的生命都在热烈地生长，向着「虚无」的尽头……

**Acheron:** We must pursue that primordial light.

> **黄泉：**我们追逐那最初的光。

**Acheron:** Before you go... Would you tell me your wish?

> **黄泉：**临行前，你愿意告诉我…你的愿望吗？

- ▸ First, find what I love...
- ▸ Then fight tooth and nail to keep it from being taken away...
- ▸ Even if I lose everything at the end...
- ▸ I will continue to trailblaze without hesitation.

**Acheron:** I'm glad. The answer itself doesn't matter. What matters is that you've made a decision.

> **黄泉：**我很高兴。答案并不重要，重要的是你已作出决定。

- ▸ What about your wish?

**Acheron:** Only a "name" remains... A person that I "was," and now want to "become."

> **黄泉：**只剩一个「名字」…一个我「本是」，现在却渴望「成为」的人。

- ▸ What about Firefly's wish?

**Acheron:** You're looking right at it.

> **黄泉：**你正注视着它。

**Acheron:** She is a hunter who treads upon the Finality, destined to sacrifice her own wishes to defy destiny.

> **黄泉：**她是行于「终末」的猎手，为了反抗命运，注定舍弃自己的愿望。

**Acheron:** But no matter which path you and she walk... Death will always be the destined finale and the last reunion.

> **黄泉：**但…无论你和她行于怎样的道路，死亡总是注定的终局，与最后的重逢。

**Acheron:** Are you ready? She, too, was here not long ago... You can witness her "resolve" through this.

> **黄泉：**准备好了吗？不久前，她也曾来到过这里…借此，你可以见证她的「决意」。

**Acheron:** ...Witness how that tiny spark of bug light broke free from the pull of the Nihility.

> **黄泉：**见证那一点萤火，如何挣脱「虚无」的引力。

**SAM (?):** Who were you in the past?

> **萨姆（？）：**过去，你是什么人？

**Firefly:** This scenery... am I... dead? Really... dead...?

> **流萤：**这里的景象…我…死去了吗？真正地……

**SAM (?):** Death is one step away from here. Step over this place, and you will truly embrace death. Or... you can turn back.

> **萨姆（？）：**还差一线，迈过此处，你可以真正拥抱死亡。或者，你也可以就此回头。

**Past:** Tell me, who were you in the past?

> **往昔：**告诉我，过去，你是什么人？

**Firefly:** ...

> **流萤：**……

**Firefly:** Glamoth's Iron Cavalry.

> **流萤：**格拉默铁骑。

**Past:** And who are you now?

> **往昔：**现在呢？

**Firefly:** A Stellaron Hunter.

> **流萤：**星核猎手。

**Past:** Then, who will you become in the future?

> **往昔：**那么往后，你要成为谁？

**Firefly:** ...Just an ordinary girl.

> **流萤：**…一个平凡的女孩子。

**Past:** Well then, keep walking forward. Death would suit you better.

> **往昔：**那么，继续向前走吧，死去对你来说更加合适。

**Firefly:** Never. I've fought tooth and nail to get where I am today, and this isn't over yet. Even if everything burns to ashes, I won't give up!

> **流萤：**绝不，我拼尽全力走到了今天，而这一切，还没有结束。即使一切烧绝烬尽，我也不会放弃！

**Past:** You should know that's a wish that can never come true.

> **往昔：**你应该知道，那是不可能实现的愿望。

**Past:** You couldn't become an ordinary girl even in "The Last Sweet Dream."

> **往昔：**即使在「最后的美梦」中，你也没能成为普通的少女。

**Firefly:** ...Why do you think that?

> **流萤：**…为什么这样认为？

**Past:** Entangled in The Family's conspiracy, facing the memetic entities' blades head-on, while challenging the resurrected Swarm Disaster alone, armed only with the "script" that can peer into the future...

> **往昔：**涉入家族的阴谋，直面迷因的锋刃，携着能窥视未来的「剧本」，独自一人，挑战复生的寰宇蝗灾。

**Past:** That's more than enough cards stacked against you.

> **往昔：**你要的证据数不胜数。

**Past:** Do you really think that the time you spent with (Trailblazer) proved anything?

> **往昔：**难道你认为，和开拓者度过的时光，足以说明什么吗？

**Past:** For an ordinary girl, simple happiness shouldn't just be a matter of chance.

> **往昔：**对于一个普通的少女，平凡的幸福不该仅仅是偶然。

**Firefly:** ...

> **流萤：**……

**Firefly:** But who's to say... all of this isn't what an ordinary girl would do?

> **流萤：**可谁说…这一切，不是一个普通少女的作为？

**Past:** ...What?

> **往昔：**…什么？

**Firefly:** Perhaps the Dreammaster was right. All the past we've left behind has already determined our future.

> **流萤：**梦主或许没有说错，所有我们抛在身后的过去，早已决定了我们的未来。

**Firefly:** All my experiences so far will still make me choose to step into danger when I face it in the future.

> **流萤：**我至今为止的经历，以后，还是会让我在面对危险时，选择踏入其中。

**Firefly:** But you misunderstood my answer.

> **流萤：**但你误会了我的话。

**Firefly:** I guess it's my fault too, always talking about wanting a normal life... As if the past that shaped me is some heavy burden weighing me down.

> **流萤：**倒也怪我，总是说着什么，想过普通的生活…好像塑造了我的过去，对我来说是沉重的负担一样。

**Firefly:** Being a Glamoth's Iron Cavalry and having Entropy Loss Syndrome are more than just scars... They are a past that taught me to cherish, urged me to seek, and showed me how to love.

> **流萤：**无论格拉默铁骑的身份，还是失熵症…都不仅仅是伤痕而已。正是这样的过去…教会我珍惜，催促我找寻，让我懂得，如何去爱。

**Firefly:** All the things you listed are quite extraordinary indeed, but I believe...

> **流萤：**你刚才所说的一切，的确很不同寻常啦，但我相信……

**Firefly:** Any ordinary girl would make the same choice I did... When faced with everything I have faced.

> **流萤：**任何一个平凡的少女，面对我所面对的这一切，也会做出一样的选择。

**Firefly:** In the darkest moments, ordinary people never need to feel small. After all, they always discover that they've already become something noble and bright.

> **流萤：**在面对黑暗的时刻，平凡人从来不需要感到卑微——毕竟，他们总是发现，自己早已是如此崇高与光明。

**Firefly:** Indeed. I can't change the outcome or even the process...

> **流萤：**是啊，我改变不了结果，甚至也改变不了过程……

**Firefly:** But I can decide how to view my destiny.

> **流萤：**但我可以决定，应该如何看待我的命运。

**Past:** This is just an even deeper form of self-deception compared to actually changing the outcome.

> **往昔：**比起能够改变结果，这只是程度更深的自欺欺人。

**Firefly:** Free will is mere self-deception for most people anyway. But as long as I'm not asking others to do the same, there's no shame in it for me.

> **流萤：**对大多数人来说，自由意志原本就是自欺欺人。但只要我没有要求别人这样做，我就不会为此感到羞愧。

**Firefly:** Everyone has their own answer as to why life slumbers.

> **流萤：**生命因何而沉睡，每个人都拥有不同的答案。

**Past:** Some will never find their answer.

> **往昔：**可有些人永远无法找到。

**Firefly:** But no one can take away their chance to keep looking for the answer.

> **流萤：**但也没有任何人，可以剥夺他们继续寻找的机会。

**Firefly:** I won't give up, nor will I blame those who do. All I need to do is return their right to make that choice... and return that right to myself as well.

> **流萤：**我仍然不会放弃，也不会指责放弃的人——我要做的，只是把选择的权力，重新交给他们，也重新交给自己。

**Firefly:** So, I will leave this place, together with you.

> **流萤：**所以，我会离开这里，和你一起。

**Firefly:** ...Even if embracing my former strength means speeding towards my death.

> **流萤：**即使拥抱曾经的力量，会让我更快死去。

**Firefly:** The name I gave myself is "Firefly." I used to think I was similar to a firefly, just a fleeting glimmer of light...

> **流萤：**我赋予自己的名字，是「流萤」。以前我还以为，是我觉得自己和萤火虫很像，只是转瞬即逝的微光……

**Firefly:** But later, I realized no one would give themselves an unlucky name.

> **流萤：**可后来我才发现，哪有人会给自己起一个不吉利的名字呀？

**Firefly:** It was also because I thought fireflies were beautiful. Later, someone told me that my eyes don't actually work very well. Otherwise, I would have figured out that those insects aren't beautiful at all.

> **流萤：**其实，原因是我觉得萤火虫很美。后来有人告诉我，那只是因为我的眼睛机能不够，否则就会发现，它们其实一点也不漂亮。

**Firefly:** Beauty... Love... All our feelings stem from the "flaws" we were born with, and that's what makes each of us unique.

> **流萤：**美也好，爱也好，我们的所有感受，来源都是我们生来的「缺陷」，而每个人都因此与众不同。

**Firefly:** Yet I can't help but love these little things.

> **流萤：**可是，我仍然会去喜欢类似的小东西。

> **流萤：**就算我很怕冷，总是受不了夜风，我也还是会去看星星。

> **流萤：**就算做蛋糕真的很麻烦，我也还是会去尝试，因为我喜欢把它送给重要的人们，看着他们微笑。

> 世界：Penacony　|　区域：<!--The Reverie (Reality)-->　|　角色：Firefly; Silver Wolf; Acheron; The Dahlia; Trailblazer; Robin; Terminus; Dr. Ratio; Aventurine; Black Swan; Jade; Dan Heng; March 7th; Welt; Himeko; Topaz and Numby; Argenti; Boothill; Old Oti; Sparkle　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Every Dawn of the World'' completed

**The Dahlia:** Wake up. It's time for this dream to end.

> **大丽花：**你看到的，都是我机缘巧合得到的「剧本」，它记载了确切发生过的一切——而借助它的刺激，你竟真正以自己的视角走入回忆，将一切重新记起。

> **大丽花：**即使记忆已被焚烧殆尽，你却能再度将其回忆起来，重新珍藏于心。

- ▸ Terminus?
- ▸ A strand?

**The Dahlia:** The Stellaron Hunters described this scene to me, something I've never seen myself: When Terminus ascends to godhood once more, THEY will manifest their thousand-armed divine form, and proclaim...

> **大丽花：**我也未曾见证过，这是星核猎手为我讲述的情景。当末王再度登神之时，祂将现出千手的神相，如此宣告——

**The Dahlia:** "Now I am become Finality, lifting the wheel of time, the destroyer of worlds."

> **大丽花：**「现在我成为了终末，高举时轮，毁灭世界。」

**The Dahlia:** Then, Terminus turned into a pack of black cats, distributing every ounce of THEIR divine power between those creatures. The cats shall depart from the end of the world, going against the river of time, till the moment of the universe's birth.

> **大丽花：**此后，「末王」化身为漆黑的群猫，其中蕴含着全部的神力。它们将从世界的尽头出发，逆时而行，直至宇宙诞生的刹那。

**The Dahlia:** What you see is one of those cats. As for Elio, he is an even more special existence. Only the Stellaron Hunters know his true identity.

> **大丽花：**而你所见到的，就是其中一只。艾利欧是更加特殊的存在，只有星核猎手知道他的真面目。

- ▸ What connection do I have with the cat?

**The Dahlia:** It will tell you the truth regarding the ending you haven't yet remembered... but you must be prepared.

> **大丽花：**你还尚未回忆起的结局，它会告诉你真相——但你必须做好准备。

**The Dahlia:** What really happened is that Firefly didn't meet with anyone between the fall of The Great Septimus and when she left Penacony... Not even you.

> **大丽花：**事实上，神主日陨落后，直到离开匹诺康尼，流萤都没有去见过任何人，包括你。

**The Dahlia:** She knew how tragic her "third death" would be, so she only dared to watch you from afar and hid on her lonesome when she was about to fade away.

> **大丽花：**她知道「第三次死亡」会是多么悲惨，只敢远远地看着你，并在即将消散之时，独自躲了起来。

**The Dahlia:** But even sad memories are precious. If you can accept farewells with grace, then you'll gain the strength to create reunions.

> **大丽花：**但悲伤的记忆也同样珍贵——你若能坦然地接受分别，就能拥有创造重逢的力量。

**The Dahlia:** When you bid farewell to loved ones, you should cry while they can still hear you. That's the only way they'll know just how many people love them.

> **大丽花：**人们送别故人，要趁着他们仍能听见时，尽早哭泣——这样他们才能知道，自己被多少人爱着。

- ▸ I will find everything that was lost.
- ▸ I will prove that they existed.
- ▸ Let's begin.

**The Dahlia:** You know, I often wonder if everything I'm doing right now is just a whimsical gift from "destiny." That I haven't really decided anything, but merely being tossed around at its mercy.

> **大丽花：**知道吗？我常常在想，自己此刻的所作所为，会否是「命运」无常的恩赐，我没有决定过什么，只是被它随意摆弄着。

**The Dahlia:** But right now? I don't care. Because my joy is real. My sorrow is real.

> **大丽花：**但此时此刻，我不在乎，因为我的欣喜是真实的，我的悲伤是真实的。

**The Dahlia:** And you will be the same. Remember, if you have regrets that must be mended, then shout with all your might from the depths of your heart. Let it be passed to the end of time and make Terminus, who is about to set off, hear your call.

> **大丽花：**你也将会一样。记住，若你有着非要弥补不可的遗憾，便在心底倾尽全力去呐喊，传至时间的尽头，让将要启程的「末王」听见。

> **大丽花：**你能看见这只黑猫，说明「末王」正在与你擦肩而过。其中一只的力量无法改变太多事，但你的过去，却是末王的未来。

> **大丽花：**它若是记住你的声音，决定在自己的未来改变些什么，你的过去也将随之改变。

> **流萤：**我们来到匹诺康尼时，梦境就已经开始与现实融合了。我以为自己在做梦，但同时，也行走于清醒的现实——这一样会让失熵症继续恶化。

**Firefly:** But don't worry. I asked Silver Wolf about it, and she said things won't get that serious in real life. Let's not talk about this anymore.

> **流萤：**不过你别担心，我问过银狼了，现实里不会这么严重，就先不聊这些了。

**Firefly:** I just don't want to see you looking sad. This is our most beautiful daydream, and we should see it through with smiles on our faces.

> **流萤：**我就怕你露出悲伤的表情——这是我们最美好的白日梦，我们要笑着把它做完。

**Firefly:** After all... we never know when we will use up all the moments we have with someone across all space and time.

> **流萤：**毕竟，我们永远不会知道，从哪一瞬间起，我们就用完了…与某个人在这片银河间所有地方共有的…所有时间。

**Firefly:** Hey, (Trailblazer)... could you close your eyes?

> **流萤：**对了，开拓者…你可不可以闭上眼睛？

**Firefly:** If this is my only chance to dream, then I want you... to see the best version of me.

> **流萤：**如果是唯一一次做梦，我希望…你能看到最好的我。

**Firefly:** As we near the end, let me indulge in a bit of perfectionism.

> **流萤：**在快要结束的时候，就让我稍微完美主义一下下吧。

**Firefly:** Just like what I used to do, let's close our eyes and imagine ourselves being together... Even if you can't see me, you'll still know that we've never been far apart.

> **流萤：**像我以前常常做的那样，闭上眼睛，去想象我们待在一起的景象…就算看不见我，但你还是可以知道，我们的距离从未遥远过。

- ▸ Don't you dare sneak away.

**Firefly:** I won't. I promise to stay as long as I can.

> **流萤：**不会的，只要我能做到，就永远不会。

- ▸ (closes eyes)

**Firefly:** Hmm... let me think... where should we start...

> **流萤：**嗯…我想想…该从哪里开始呢……

**Firefly:** Right. We should start "where everything began," of course.

> **流萤：**对啦，当然要从「开始的地方」开始。

**Firefly:** Right at that Golden Hour, when you came to save me...

> **流萤：**就在黄金的时刻，你来救我的时候——

**Firefly:** That being said, we could also pretend we're strangers who just happened to meet there.

> **流萤：**不过，也可以假设我们素不相识，只是偶然在那里相遇。

**Firefly:** Me... I'm a student traveling to the Golden Hour, and it'd be nice if I had a bit more pocket money... Oh, and when I show up there, I should dress up a little fancier.

> **流萤：**我嘛…是一个来黄金时刻旅行的学生，最好零花钱能稍稍多一点…然后我出现在那里时，打扮得也要精致一点。

**Firefly:** And you... You're a student too! But you came here from a really, really distant planet, completely unfamiliar with your surroundings.

> **流萤：**你…你也是学生！但却是从好远好远的一颗星球上来到这里，完全不熟悉环境。

**Firefly:** Then, a bunch of mean-looking thugs jumped out, trying to pick on you.

> **流萤：**然后，就跳出来几个好凶好凶的流氓，想要欺负你。

**Firefly:** When that happens, I'll step up to the plate. Yeah! Let me be the one to save you this time.

> **流萤：**而那时候，我会挺身而出——对，这一次，让我来拯救你。

- ▸ We can't beat them. Let's run away together.
- ▸ Don't worry about me. I don't want you getting hurt because of me.
- ▸ Why don't we team up and fight together?

**Firefly:** No worries. That's exactly what I'm going to do.

> **流萤：**没关系，我就是要那样做。

**Firefly:** The result doesn't matter, and even the process isn't important. What matters is how we take part in this journey and how we choose to view it.

> **流萤：**结果并不重要，甚至过程也不重要。重要的是：我们怎样参与这趟旅程，又如何去看待它。

**Firefly:** So, (Trailblazer), grant me one more wish...

> **流萤：**所以，开拓者，再答应我一个愿望……

**Firefly:** Remember how I held you and flew into the sky when we fought against Ena's Dream? Back then, I wasn't sure if I would ever wake up again, but I was happy.

> **流萤：**还记得吗？在对抗「太一之梦」的时候，我抱着你飞上天空。那时，我不知道自己还能否醒来，但我却很开心。

**Firefly:** We were standing shoulder to shoulder once again, fighting against things we believed were "wrong," and we'd do anything for each other.

> **流萤：**因为，我们又能肩并肩，去对抗某些我们认为「不对」的东西，并且，能为对方不顾一切。

**Firefly:** Still, I have a tiny, selfish wish. I know you won't forget this moment, but when you look back on it, I want it to feel lighter.

> **流萤：**不过，我还是有一点小小的私心——我相信你不会忘记这一幕，但如果回想起来，我想让它轻松一点。

**Firefly:** Maybe there were no enemies at all. It was all just a prank. And when I carried you up into the heavens, what we saw there wasn't the terrifying Swarm, but rather... the fireworks that I never got to see.

> **流萤：**也许没有什么敌人，一切只是场恶作剧。而我抱着你飞上天空时，天空中不是可怕的虫群，而是…我没能看到的烟花。

- ▸ You'll be able to see the fireworks.
- ▸ I'll remember you just the way you want me to.
- ▸ You've already seen it, you dummy!

...

...

- ▸ Firefly?
- ▸ I'm gonna open my eyes.
- ▸ ...

**(Trailblazer):** ...Firefly!?

> **开拓者：**…流萤？！

**Firefly:** (Trailblazer)... Elio said we're destined to head to different finalities, that we can't have a future.

> **流萤：**开拓者…艾利欧说，我们注定走向不同的终末，不可能拥有未来。

**Firefly:** That's why you told me that you'll give me a daydream more beautiful than any ending could ever be.

> **流萤：**所以你说，你会给我一个，比任何结局都美好的白日梦。

**Firefly:** I'm looking forward to it, but... can we really... not step over that ending together?

> **流萤：**我很期待…但是，我们难道真的就不能，一起跨过那个结局吗？

**Firefly:** I'm not scared of death... but please, don't let me die alone...

> **流萤：**我不害怕死…但不要让我独自一人死去……

**Firefly:** They say... you're destined to bring about the Finality.

> **流萤：**他们都说…你是注定带来「终末」的人。

**Firefly:** But I believe you'll be there... You definitely will...

> **流萤：**但相信你会在的…一定会……

**Firefly:** Only you can know it and only you can decide. This cosmos certainly doesn't exist solely for you, nor does it flow only under your gaze... But it's forever waiting for you to save it.

> **流萤：**只有你能知道，只有你能决定。这片银河，的确不是只为你而存在，也不是只在你的注视下流淌…但它永远等待着你的拯救。

"Those afflicted with the Entropy Loss Syndrome will experience an irreversible chronic dissolution of their physical form, gradually fading away. You can still run, jump, and talk with others. Everything seems normal, except you're always just a little slower than everyone else..."

"Until the outlines of yourself and the entire world blur into obscurity. You can no longer distinguish reality from dreams, for both have become equally fractured."

She is nearing the end stage of the Entropy Loss, her mind already splintered first.

And as she gradually forgot everything, the story of you was the last to fade.

**(Trailblazer):** Even so, I'll still be the last thing you forget...

> **开拓者：**就算如此，你也是到最后，才会忘记我……

The dissolution is not yet over. And in this very instant, her mind holds only "one name" and "one wish."

**Firefly:** (Trailblazer)... Fireworks...

> **流萤：**开拓者…烟花……

**(Trailblazer):** (No. While she might still be able to see it...)

> **开拓者：**（不，趁着她也许还能看见……）

- ▸ Hold her hand and fulfill her wish.

**(Trailblazer):** The result doesn't matter, and even the process isn't important. What matters is how we take part in this journey and how we choose to view it.

> **开拓者：**结果并不重要，甚至过程也不重要。重要的是：我们怎样参与这趟旅程，又如何去看待它。

**(Trailblazer):** I'll do everything I promised you.

> **开拓者：**答应你的，我都会做到。

However, Firefly's eyes never move again. Every spark of life in her eyes has already gone.

**(Trailblazer):** Am I... still one step too late...

> **开拓者：**我…还是慢了一步吗……

It is too late. You would rather remember her in the way she wished, and in your mind, the ending would be a happy one.

But when she looks back on this day, she will always discover one regret that can never be mended.

**(Trailblazer):** Why...

> **开拓者：**为什么……

The crowd buzzes with excitement and the fireworks are about to light up the sky...

**(Trailblazer):** But Firefly can't see it anymore...

> **开拓者：**可流萤已经看不见了……

And a song comes on the wind...

"♪ Will she be clearer~ ♪♪~ because she is gone forever?"

"♪ The bouquet you gave me~ ♪♪~ can the sound of its petals falling keep me awake always?"

**(Trailblazer):** No... It's not beyond saving...

> **开拓者：**不，不是无法弥补……

**(Trailblazer):** I can change the course of destiny and make up for what's missing. If the world has set an endpoint for our destiny, then at the very least... it owes Firefly the bouquet she wanted.

> **开拓者：**我能够拨转命运，成全缺憾。世界给我们的命运，立下一个终点，那它至少，要补偿给流萤…她想要的花束。

The voice at your ear blurs, then fades into silence...

You recall the fireworks in your memory, where Firefly caught you as you fell from the sky and carried you up into the heavens. At that moment, the words she spoke left a perfect footnote for the story, one belonging to her, and to everyone under the night sky.

And you know that this footnote no longer exists.

The power of Finality is turning back time, and the girl's final regret is within sight. "A beautiful daydream..." The moment to fulfill that promise has come. You can, you will, you must... make it a reality.

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Firefly:** You see, everything is possible in this land of dreams.

> **流萤：**你看，在这片梦想之地，一切都被允许，一切都有可能。

**Firefly:** We each came here with our own goals, and realized them in unimaginable ways.

> **流萤：**我们怀着各自的目的来到这里，又以意想不到的方式实现了它。

**Firefly:** Whether the outcome was sweet and dreamlike, or bitter and real, it is still the answer we longed for.

> **流萤：**无论那结果是甜美的、梦幻的，还是苦涩的、现实的，它都是我们梦寐以求的答案。

**Firefly:** So why do people choose to sleep? I think it's as you said...

> **流萤：**所以，人们为何选择沉睡？我想，就像你说的一样……

**Firefly:** So when tomorrow comes, we'll wake from our dreams smiling.

> **流萤：**为了明天，能够笑着从梦中醒来。

*(Cutscene ends)*

### Complete the ending of the sweet dream

> *You can't return to the past, yet the past, regardless of whether you changed it, will leave traces to be remembered.*

*(Investigate the photo in the Parlor Car)*

*(Quick Read)*

Dream's Curtain Call

A photo taken at an unknown time, but the reality of its touch is undeniable. In the past, this scene truly came to pass, leaving behind a proof of its transpiration. Though few are aware, a young girl will smile when she recalls a beautiful dream she once had. The dreamer is also the dream-maker. The dream-maker is also the dreamer. Only by offering up one's past can the most beautiful dreams be woven. People will not lose themselves within them, unwilling to awaken. In their beautiful dreams, they will regain the courage to face the future. And then, walk toward tomorrow.

In their beautiful dreams, they will regain the courage to face the future. And then, walk toward tomorrow.
